9	
10	# Desktop Pre-Release Batch Implementation Plan
11	
12	## Overview
13	
14	Get `apps/desktop` from "quick-wins applied" to "first public `@lightfast/desktop@0.1.0` release shipping a signed, notarized, auto-updating macOS binary with Sentry-readable stack traces." Six phases. Phase A is a human-setup checklist; B–F are code/CI changes. All four prior quick-win phases are already applied and verified in the current tree.
15	
16	## Current State Analysis
17	
18	All four phases of `2026-04-23-desktop-codex-gap-quick-wins.md` are landed:
19	- Entitlements trimmed (`build/entitlements.mac.plist` has 5 keys; over-broad `disable-library-validation`/`device.camera`/`network.server` removed).
20	- `forge.config.ts:67-78` has `NSQuitAlwaysKeepsWindows=false`, `LSMinimumSystemVersion=12.0`, `LSEnvironment.MallocNanoZone="0"`.
21	- `src/main/sentry.ts:14-55` has `SESSION_ID`, `rewriteFramesIntegration({root, prefix: "app:///"})`, `dist=buildNumber`, `bundle/host/sessionId` tags.
22	- `showContextMenu` IPC, `silentRefresh`, and the deep-link `console.log` are gone. `protocol.ts` deleted; OAuth moved to a loopback HTTP server (`auth-flow.ts:70-141`).
23	
24	What still blocks a v0.1.0 release:
25	- `.github/workflows/desktop-release.yml.disabled` is inert (correctly, since Apple signing secrets aren't provisioned yet).
26	- `forge.config.ts:43-54` reads `LIGHTFAST_DESKTOP_RELEASE_REPO` but the disabled workflow passes `${{ github.repository }}` — two conflicting paths.
27	- `@sentry/cli` is in `pnpm-workspace.yaml:82` `onlyBuiltDependencies` but no script invokes it; packaged-build stack traces will be unreadable despite `rewriteFramesIntegration` being wired.
28	- `ci-core.yml:45,105` explicitly excludes desktop from typecheck + build. `ci.yml:50-54` only runs typecheck via `turbo --affected`. Nothing runs `electron-forge package` on PRs.
29	- `apps/desktop/package.json` placeholders: `version: "0.0.0"`, empty `sparkleFeedUrl`, unused `sparklePublicKey: ""`.
30	- No `.env.example`, no `lint`/`clean` scripts in desktop, `dev:desktop-stack` is misnamed at the root.
31	
32	### Key Discoveries
33	
34	- `sparklePublicKey` is declared in `src/shared/env.ts:12`, `src/shared/ipc.ts:52`, and `src/main/build-info.ts:23` but has **zero** downstream readers (grep confirms). Electron's `autoUpdater` does not use it. Drop the field instead of stamping it in CI.
35	- Sentry DSNs are public identifiers (they're visible in any shipped app's JS bundle). Baking at CI build time via `npm pkg set sentryDsn=...` from a secret is simplest — no runtime env plumbing required on macOS.
36	- Release tag convention in this repo is already `<package>@<version>` (changesets produces `lightfast@x.y.z`, `@lightfastai/mcp@x.y.z` via `release.yml`). Desktop should match: `@lightfast/desktop@<version>`. YAML trigger: `'@lightfast/desktop@*'`.
37	- Desktop is `"private": true`; `changesets/action` ignores private packages automatically — it cannot and will not try to publish desktop. The blocker isn't publishing, it's that `verify-changeset.yml:51` rejects any changeset file mentioning `@lightfast/desktop`. We stay tag-only and add the package to `.changeset/config.json` `ignore` so `pnpm changeset` never prompts for it.
38	- Monorepo is public (`gh repo view` → `"visibility":"PUBLIC"`). Sparkle feed URL `github.com/lightfastai/lightfast/releases/latest/download/latest-mac-${arch}.json` works without auth.
39	- `.vite/build` (main + preload) and `.vite/renderer/main_window` (renderer) are the two source-map roots `sentry-cli upload-sourcemaps --url-prefix "app:///"` needs to target — both bundles end up addressed via the `app:///` prefix by `rewriteFramesIntegration`.
40	
41	## Desired End State
42	
43	1. `git tag @lightfast/desktop@0.1.0 && git push origin '@lightfast/desktop@0.1.0'` fires `desktop-release.yml`, which builds signed+notarized arm64 and x64 `.zip` + `.dmg`, uploads source maps to Sentry, publishes GitHub release assets with Squirrel.Mac JSON feeds, and undrafts the release.
44	2. Packaged app's Sentry issues have fully symbolicated stacks (file/line → repo file/line), session tag, `dist`, and the `@lightfast/desktop@0.1.0+<runNumber>` release id.
45	3. Every PR touching `apps/desktop/**` runs typecheck + `electron-forge package` on macOS and fails if either breaks.
46	4. A new contributor runs `cp apps/desktop/.env.example apps/desktop/.env.development && pnpm dev:desktop` and the app boots.
47	5. `pnpm changeset` in the repo never prompts for `@lightfast/desktop`.
48	
49	Verification:
50	- `gh release view '@lightfast/desktop@0.1.0' --repo lightfastai/lightfast` shows 6 assets (arm64 zip+dmg+json, x64 zip+dmg+json) plus undraft state.
51	- `sentry-cli releases info '@lightfast/desktop@0.1.0+<runNumber>' --project lightfast-desktop` lists uploaded source maps.
52	- `.github/workflows/desktop-release.yml` exists and is green on the tag push.
53	- `pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop lint` passes.
54	
55	## What We're NOT Doing
56	
57	- Sparkle-native / Ed25519 auto-update (Squirrel.Mac is fine for v0.1.0). `sparklePublicKey` gets deleted, not stamped.
58	- Windows + Linux packaging (`MakerSquirrel` stays in `forge.config.ts:82-85` dormant; no Linux makers; no Windows job in the workflow).
59	- Universal binaries (`@electron/universal`). Two separate `-arm64` / `-x64` artifacts are published; the existing `${arch}` template in `updater.ts:27-29` handles dispatch.
60	- File-backed structured logger. Sentry + breadcrumbs cover v0.1.0; revisit once a user-issue-debugging workflow demands it.
61	- SQLite / persistent structured storage, worker tier, per-surface preload isolation, renderer i18n, Playwright harness, notifications — all deferred per original research `Status Tracker`.
62	- Wiring desktop into the changeset-driven release flow (`release.yml`). Desktop stays tag-only.
63	- A cross-repo `lightfastai/lightfast-desktop-releases` release host. Monorepo tags only.
64	
65	## Implementation Approach
66	
67	Strict phase order because of dependencies:
68	
69	- **Phase A (setup)** must happen before D can be tested end-to-end. Runs in parallel with B/C/E/F code work.
70	- **Phase B** must land before D (D depends on Vite's DSN injection from B).
71	- **Phase C** adds the script that D calls.
72	- **Phases E and F** are independent — can land in either order relative to B–D.
73	
74	### Prerequisites (outside this plan)
75	
76	- `thoughts/shared/plans/2026-04-24-coderabbit-pr614-fixes.md` — resolve the port-collision and `will-navigate` guard items flagged against the loopback OAuth flow in PR #614 before Phase D's end-to-end validation. An unsigned release that crashes on a port conflict during sign-in will look worse than not releasing at all.
77	
78	---
79	
80	## Phase A: External Setup Checklist (human)
81	
82	### Overview
83	
84	No code. Provision the four external resources the workflow depends on, and commit secrets to GitHub. Skipping any item here will leave `desktop-release.yml` failing on the first run.
85	
86	### Apple Developer (8 secrets)
87	
88	1. Enroll in **Apple Developer Program** (~$99/yr) under the Lightfast business entity. Capture the 10-character **Team ID** once enrolled.
89	2. Generate a **Developer ID Application** certificate in developer.apple.com → Certificates. Download the `.cer`, add to Keychain Access on a Mac, export the matching identity as a password-protected `.p12`.
90	3. Generate an **App Store Connect API key** with "Developer" role (developer.apple.com → Users and Access → Keys). Download the `.p8` (one-time download). Capture the **Key ID** (10-char) and **Issuer ID** (UUID).
91	4. In repo settings → Secrets and variables → Actions, add these secrets:
92	   - `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY` — full identity string, e.g. `Developer ID Application: Lightfast AI Inc (ABC1234567)`
93	   - `APPLE_TEAM_ID` — 10-char team id (e.g. `ABC1234567`)
94	   - `APPLE_CERT_BASE64` — output of `base64 < DeveloperID.p12 | pbcopy` (macOS) or `base64 -w0 DeveloperID.p12` (Linux)
95	   - `APPLE_CERT_PASSWORD` — the p12 password chosen at export
96	   - `APPLE_API_KEY_ID` — the 10-char key id from step 3
97	   - `APPLE_API_ISSUER` — the issuer UUID from step 3
98	   - `APPLE_API_KEY_CONTENT` — raw contents of the `.p8` file (including the `-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----` header/footer)
99	   - `KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD` — any strong random string (e.g. `openssl rand -base64 24`); used only inside the CI keychain
100	
101	### Sentry (2 secrets + 1 project)
102	
103	1. In Sentry, create a new **Electron** project named `lightfast-desktop` under the existing Lightfast org. Capture the DSN.
104	2. Create a Sentry **organization auth token** with `project:releases` scope (Settings → Developer Settings → Auth Tokens).
105	3. Add these secrets to the repo:
106	   - `SENTRY_DSN` — the DSN from step 1 (public, but storing as a secret keeps it out of git)
107	   - `SENTRY_AUTH_TOKEN` — the auth token from step 2
108	4. Add these repo variables (Variables tab, not Secrets):
109	   - `SENTRY_ORG` — Sentry org slug (e.g. `lightfast`)
110	   - `SENTRY_PROJECT` — `lightfast-desktop`
111	
112	### Success Criteria
113	
114	Nothing to automate. Cross off the 11 items above; confirm by visiting `github.com/lightfastai/lightfast/settings/secrets/actions` and seeing all 8 Apple secrets + 2 Sentry secrets + 2 Sentry variables present.
115	
116	**Stop here for human confirmation that Phase A is complete before starting Phase D validation.**
117	
118	---
119	
120	## Phase B: Env layer overhaul + forge config + sparkle cleanup
121	
122	### Overview
123	
124	Four cleanups, with the env layer as the load-bearing change:
125	
126	1. **New t3-env-based env layer** at `apps/desktop/src/env/` — replaces the silent-fail `parseRuntimeEnv` in `src/shared/env.ts` with two `createEnv` calls (main, renderer). Matches the repo convention used by every other workspace.
127	2. **Sentry DSN baked via Vite `define` with a custom token** (`__SENTRY_DSN__`) — not via `process.env.SENTRY_DSN` replacement. Sentry's official Electron troubleshooting docs warn against replacing `process.*` keys in Vite `define` because it can break Sentry SDK internals. Custom token pattern avoids the conflict.
128	3. **Forge config simplification** — hardcode the release repo, remove the `LIGHTFAST_DESKTOP_RELEASE_REPO` indirection.
129	4. **Sparkle cleanup** — drop the unused `sparklePublicKey` field end-to-end.
130	
131	**Spike verdict (CONFIRMED, 2026-04-24)**: `@t3-oss/env-core` (ESM-only) inlines cleanly into Forge's main-process CJS bundle at package time. `ssr.noExternal` is NOT needed — Forge's `@electron-forge/plugin-vite@7.11.1` bundles everything except electron + `node:*` builtins by default. Custom-token `define` works as expected. See Improvement Log.
132	
133	**Why this matters now (not deferred)**: Today's `parseRuntimeEnv` silently returns `{}` on validation failure (`apps/desktop/src/shared/env.ts:26-31`). A packaged app with a missing/malformed env var boots with wrong defaults and Sentry silently doesn't initialize. This is the opposite of the "build-fail-fast" behavior that makes `apps/www/next.config.ts` safe. Shipping v0.1.0 with this behavior turns every future env-touching change into a landmine.
134	
135	### Changes Required
136	
137	#### 1. `apps/desktop/package.json` — add `@t3-oss/env-core`
138	
139	**File**: `apps/desktop/package.json` (devDependencies, alphabetical insertion)
140	**Changes**: Add `"@t3-oss/env-core": "catalog:"` (already pinned to `^0.13.11` in `pnpm-workspace.yaml`). Run `pnpm install` at repo root to refresh `pnpm-lock.yaml`.
141	
142	Rationale: every other workspace (`vendor/db`, `vendor/inngest`, `vendor/upstash`, `core/cli`, `db/app`, `packages/app-providers`) uses `@t3-oss/env-core` for non-Next.js contexts. Desktop is the only outlier with a hand-rolled schema. Adopting the catalog dep is one line.
143	
144	#### 2. `apps/desktop/src/env/main.ts` (new file)
145	
146	**File**: `apps/desktop/src/env/main.ts`
147	**Changes**: New file. Single source of truth for main-process env vars. Build-fail-fast via t3-env.
148	
149	```ts
150	import { createEnv } from "@t3-oss/env-core";
151	import { z } from "zod";
152	
153	// Populated by Vite's `define` at package time (see vite.main.config.ts).
154	// `undefined` guard covers `pnpm dev` (define is still applied) and
155	// ad-hoc `node` invocations during tooling.
156	declare const __SENTRY_DSN__: string | undefined;
157	
158	const buildFlavor = z.enum(["dev", "preview", "prod"]);
159	
160	export const mainEnv = createEnv({
161	  server: {
162	    // Sentry (baked at build time via Vite define; optional in dev).
163	    SENTRY_DSN: z.string().url().optional(),
164	    // API origin. Main-process side (auth-flow loopback, CSP allowlist).
165	    LIGHTFAST_API_URL: z
166	      .string()
167	      .url()
168	      .default("http://localhost:3024"),
169	    // Clerk frontend API — used by index.ts to compute the CSP `connect-src`
170	    // host for the Clerk iframe. Required (sign-in breaks otherwise).
171	    NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY: z
172	      .string()
173	      .min(1, "Missing NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY"),
174	    // Build-info overrides (used by build-info.ts).
175	    BUILD_FLAVOR: buildFlavor.optional(),
176	    SPARKLE_FEED_URL: z.string().url().optional(),
177	    SQUIRREL_FEED_URL: z.string().url().optional(),
178	    // Dev-only remote debugging.
179	    LIGHTFAST_REMOTE_DEBUG_PORT: z.coerce
180	      .number()
181	      .int()
182	      .min(1)
183	      .max(65535)
184	      .optional(),
185	  },
186	  runtimeEnv: {
187	    SENTRY_DSN:
188	      typeof __SENTRY_DSN__ !== "undefined" && __SENTRY_DSN__ !== ""
189	        ? __SENTRY_DSN__
190	        : process.env.SENTRY_DSN,
191	    LIGHTFAST_API_URL: process.env.LIGHTFAST_API_URL,
192	    NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY:
193	      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY,
194	    BUILD_FLAVOR: process.env.BUILD_FLAVOR,
195	    SPARKLE_FEED_URL: process.env.SPARKLE_FEED_URL,
196	    SQUIRREL_FEED_URL: process.env.SQUIRREL_FEED_URL,
197	    LIGHTFAST_REMOTE_DEBUG_PORT: process.env.LIGHTFAST_REMOTE_DEBUG_PORT,
198	  },
199	  isServer: true,
200	  emptyStringAsUndefined: true,
201	  skipValidation: !!process.env.SKIP_ENV_VALIDATION,
202	});
203	```
204	
205	Notes:
206	- `isServer: true` is explicit because t3-env's default heuristic (`typeof window === "undefined"`) misfires in Electron's preload context (preload has `window` but is Node-side).
207	- `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` keeps its Next.js-style name because that's what the rest of the monorepo sets (`apps/app`, `apps/www`); renaming would fragment the contributor mental model. It's server-only in the desktop schema because only main reads it (for CSP).
208	- `default()` on `LIGHTFAST_API_URL` collapses the duplicated `getApiOrigin()` helpers in `index.ts:45-51` and `auth-flow.ts:10-16` into a single validated value.
209	
210	#### 3. `apps/desktop/src/env/renderer.ts` (new file)
211	
212	**File**: `apps/desktop/src/env/renderer.ts`
213	**Changes**: New file. Renderer env validation with Vite's `VITE_*` prefix.
214	
215	```ts
216	import { createEnv } from "@t3-oss/env-core";
217	import { z } from "zod";
218	
219	export const rendererEnv = createEnv({
220	  clientPrefix: "VITE_",
221	  client: {
222	    VITE_LIGHTFAST_API_URL: z
223	      .string()
224	      .url()
225	      .default("http://localhost:3024"),
226	  },
227	  runtimeEnv: import.meta.env,
228	  emptyStringAsUndefined: true,
229	  skipValidation: !!import.meta.env.VITE_SKIP_ENV_VALIDATION,
230	});
231	```
232	
233	Only `VITE_LIGHTFAST_API_URL` is validated because that's the only `import.meta.env` access in the renderer tree today (`entry.tsx:7`). Adding schema slots for vars nothing reads ("future-proofing") accumulates tech debt identical to what we're ripping out — add them in the PR that introduces the consumer.
234	
235	#### 4. `apps/desktop/vite.main.config.ts`
236	
237	**File**: `apps/desktop/vite.main.config.ts`
238	**Changes**: Add `define` with a **custom token** (not `process.env.SENTRY_DSN`). Per Sentry's official Electron troubleshooting: replacing `process.*` keys in Vite `define` can break the Sentry SDK's internal `process.env` references.
239	
240	```ts
241	export default defineConfig({
242	  define: {
243	    __SENTRY_DSN__: JSON.stringify(process.env.SENTRY_DSN ?? ""),
244	  },
245	  // ...existing...
246	});
247	```
248	
249	#### 5. `apps/desktop/src/main/sentry.ts`
250	
251	**File**: `apps/desktop/src/main/sentry.ts`
252	**Changes**: Read DSN + build-flavor check via `mainEnv` instead of `getRuntimeEnv()`.
253	
254	```ts
255	// Before (line 1):
256	import { getBuildInfo, getRuntimeEnv } from "./build-info";
257	
258	// After:
259	import { mainEnv } from "../env/main";
260	import { getBuildInfo } from "./build-info";
261	
262	export function getSentryInitOptions(): SentryInitOptions {
263	  const build = getBuildInfo();
264	  const dsn = mainEnv.SENTRY_DSN ?? "";
265	  return {
266	    dsn,
267	    release: `${build.name}@${build.version}+${build.buildNumber}`,
268	    environment: build.buildFlavor,
269	    enabled: Boolean(dsn) && build.buildFlavor !== "dev",
270	  };
271	}
272	```

473	## Phase C: Sentry source-map upload script
474	
475	### Overview
476	
477	Add a `scripts/upload-sourcemaps.mjs` that wraps `@sentry/cli` and uploads both `.vite/build` (main + preload) and `.vite/renderer/main_window` (renderer) with `--url-prefix "app:///"` so uploaded maps align with the `rewriteFramesIntegration` prefix set in `sentry.ts:45`. Add `@sentry/cli` to desktop devDeps so the script can invoke it via `pnpm exec sentry-cli`.
478	
479	### Changes Required
480	
481	#### 1. `apps/desktop/package.json`
482	
483	**File**: `apps/desktop/package.json` (devDependencies, alphabetical insertion)
484	**Changes**: Add `@sentry/cli` to devDependencies; add a `sourcemaps:upload` script.
485	
486	```json
487	"scripts": {
488	  "dev": "electron-forge start",
489	  "package": "electron-forge package",
490	  "make": "electron-forge make",
491	  "publish": "electron-forge publish",
492	  "sourcemaps:upload": "node scripts/upload-sourcemaps.mjs",
493	  "typecheck": "tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json && tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.node.json"
494	},
495	```
496	
497	Add `"@sentry/cli": "^2.39.1"` to `devDependencies` (alphabetical slot between `@sentry/electron` and `@tanstack/query-core`). Then run `pnpm install` from repo root to refresh `pnpm-lock.yaml`.
498	
499	#### 2. `apps/desktop/scripts/upload-sourcemaps.mjs` (new file)
500	
501	**File**: `apps/desktop/scripts/upload-sourcemaps.mjs`
502	**Changes**: New file.
503	
504	```js
505	#!/usr/bin/env node
506	import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
507	import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
508	import { dirname, resolve } from "node:path";
509	import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
510	
511	const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
512	const desktopRoot = resolve(here, "..");
513	const pkg = JSON.parse(
514	  readFileSync(resolve(desktopRoot, "package.json"), "utf8")
515	);
516	
517	const required = ["SENTRY_AUTH_TOKEN", "SENTRY_ORG", "SENTRY_PROJECT"];
518	for (const name of required) {
519	  if (!process.env[name]) {
520	    console.error(`Missing env ${name}`);
521	    process.exit(1);
522	  }
523	}
524	
525	const release = `${pkg.name}@${pkg.version}+${pkg.buildNumber}`;
526	const urlPrefix = "app:///";
527	const buildDir = resolve(desktopRoot, ".vite/build");
528	const rendererDir = resolve(desktopRoot, ".vite/renderer/main_window");
529	
530	function sentry(args) {
531	  execFileSync("pnpm", ["exec", "sentry-cli", ...args], {
532	    cwd: desktopRoot,
533	    stdio: "inherit",
534	    env: process.env,
535	  });
536	}
537	
538	sentry(["releases", "new", release]);
539	sentry([
540	  "releases",
541	  "files",
542	  release,
543	  "upload-sourcemaps",
544	  "--url-prefix",
545	  urlPrefix,
546	  "--ext",
547	  "js",
548	  "--ext",
549	  "map",
550	  buildDir,
551	]);
552	sentry([
553	  "releases",
554	  "files",
555	  release,
556	  "upload-sourcemaps",
557	  "--url-prefix",
558	  urlPrefix,
559	  "--ext",
560	  "js",
561	  "--ext",
562	  "map",
563	  rendererDir,
564	]);
565	sentry(["releases", "finalize", release]);
566	
567	console.log(`Uploaded sourcemaps for release ${release}`);
568	```
569	
570	Why two separate upload calls: `sentry-cli upload-sourcemaps` takes one path. Calling twice is cheaper than trying to symlink or merge the two output trees.
571	
572	### Success Criteria
573	
574	#### Automated Verification
575	
576	- [ ] `pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop exec sentry-cli --version` prints a version.
577	- [ ] `pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop typecheck` still passes (no TS changes, but sanity).
578	- [ ] `node apps/desktop/scripts/upload-sourcemaps.mjs` with all three env vars missing exits 1 with a clear message.
579	
580	#### Manual Verification (requires Phase A Sentry secrets)
581	
582	- [ ] In a scratch shell: `pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop package` to populate `.vite/`, then `SENTRY_DSN=<dsn> SENTRY_ORG=<slug> REDACTED SENTRY_AUTH_TOKEN=<token> pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop sourcemaps:upload`.
583	- [ ] In Sentry UI → Releases → `@lightfast/desktop@0.0.0+1`, confirm source maps are listed under Artifacts and at least one path starts with `app:///`.
584	
585	**Stop here for manual confirmation before Phase D.**

761	
762	## Phase E: Desktop CI coverage
763	
764	### Overview
765	
766	New standalone workflow `.github/workflows/desktop-ci.yml` triggered by native `on: push: paths:` filtering on desktop-touching paths. Runs typecheck + `electron-forge package` (unsigned) on `macos-14`. No `dorny/paths-filter` dependency — matches `verify-changeset.yml`'s native pattern. No `if:` guards on every step.
767	
768	`ci.yml` is left untouched (`ci-success` does NOT depend on desktop-ci); the desktop job is a parallel required check, configured via branch protection rather than `needs:`.
769	
770	### Changes Required
771	
772	#### 1. `.github/workflows/desktop-ci.yml` (new file)
773	
774	**File**: `.github/workflows/desktop-ci.yml`
775	**Changes**: New file. Triggers only on PRs/pushes that touch desktop-relevant paths.
776	
777	```yaml
778	name: Desktop CI
779	
780	on:
781	  pull_request:
782	    paths:
783	      - 'apps/desktop/**'
784	      - 'packages/app-trpc/**'
785	      - 'packages/ui/**'
786	      - 'packages/lib/**'
787	      - 'pnpm-lock.yaml'
788	      - 'pnpm-workspace.yaml'
789	      - '.github/workflows/desktop-ci.yml'
790	  push:
791	    branches: [main]
792	    paths:
793	      - 'apps/desktop/**'
794	      - 'packages/app-trpc/**'
795	      - 'packages/ui/**'
796	      - 'packages/lib/**'
797	      - 'pnpm-lock.yaml'
798	      - 'pnpm-workspace.yaml'
799	      - '.github/workflows/desktop-ci.yml'
800	
801	concurrency:
802	  group: desktop-ci-${{ github.ref }}
803	  cancel-in-progress: true
804	
805	jobs:
806	  package:
807	    name: Typecheck + package (unsigned)
808	    runs-on: macos-14
809	    timeout-minutes: 30
810	    env:
811	      TURBO_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.TURBO_TOKEN }}
812	      TURBO_TEAM: ${{ vars.TURBO_TEAM }}
813	    steps:
814	      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
815	        with:
816	          fetch-depth: 0
817	
818	      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
819	
820	      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
821	        with:
822	          node-version: 22
823	          cache: 'pnpm'
824	
825	      - run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
826	
827	      - name: Typecheck
828	        run: pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop typecheck
829	
830	      - name: Package (unsigned)
831	        run: pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop package
832	```
833	
834	**Why standalone workflow + native `paths:`** vs. adding a job to `ci.yml`: workflow-level `paths:` filtering means GitHub never schedules a runner when the trigger doesn't match — no wasted CI minutes, no `if:` guards on individual steps, no third-party action. Matches the existing `verify-changeset.yml:12-14` pattern (the only existing `paths:`-filtered workflow in the repo).
835	
836	**Why `macos-14` + unsigned**: signing needs Apple secrets we cannot expose to PR forks. `electron-forge package` without sign/notarize still catches the 95% case — native module rebuild, forge config errors, Vite build failures, missing imports, broken IPC contracts.
837	
838	**Workspace deps included** (`@repo/app-trpc`, `@repo/ui`, `@repo/lib`): the three desktop imports most likely to break the build. `@repo/ai` is renderer-side and not in the main bundle path — out of scope. `pnpm-lock.yaml` catches transitive dep bumps. Trade-off is explicit: this catches ~80% of dep-driven regressions without becoming a "rebuild on any package change" hammer.
839	
840	#### 2. Branch protection (manual configuration)
841	
842	Add `Desktop CI / Typecheck + package (unsigned)` as a required status check on `main` in repo settings → Branches → branch protection rules. Note that this check appears only on PRs that touch desktop paths; on PRs that don't, GitHub treats the absent check as "not required" (this is GitHub's documented behavior for `paths:`-filtered workflows).
843	
844	#### 3. `.github/workflows/ci.yml` and `.github/workflows/ci-core.yml`
845	
846	**Changes**: None. `ci-core.yml` scope ("things we publish to npm") stays unchanged. `ci.yml` continues to typecheck desktop via `pnpm turbo typecheck --affected --continue` (line 54) — that's a fast Linux job and complementary to the macOS package gate.
847	
848	### Success Criteria
849	
850	#### Automated Verification
851	
852	- [ ] A PR touching only `apps/desktop/src/main/sentry.ts` triggers `Desktop CI` and skips it on PRs touching only `apps/www/**`.
853	- [ ] `gh workflow view "Desktop CI"` shows the workflow as enabled after the PR merges.
854	- [ ] The package job completes in < 20 minutes on `macos-14`.
855	
856	#### Manual Verification
857	
858	- [ ] Open a throwaway PR with a one-char change to `apps/desktop/src/main/index.ts`. Confirm `Desktop CI` runs and passes.
859	- [ ] Open a separate throwaway PR touching only `apps/www/page.tsx`. Confirm `Desktop CI` does NOT run (no wasted macOS minutes).
860	- [ ] Update branch protection to require `Desktop CI / Typecheck + package (unsigned)`. Confirm a desktop-touching PR cannot merge until the check is green.
861

863	
864	## Phase F: Contributor ergonomics
865	
866	### Overview
867	
868	Small fixes that reduce friction for anyone touching desktop: a `.env.example`, a `clean` script, the `dev:desktop-stack` naming fix, and the changesets ignore entry for `@lightfast/desktop`.
869	
870	**Not doing**: per-package `lint` script. Every other workspace in the monorepo is lint-covered by root `pnpm check` → `npx ultracite@latest check`, which walks the whole tree. Adding a `"lint": "biome check"` to `apps/desktop/package.json` would be the first per-package lint script in the repo — convention-breaking for no real benefit, and there's no `biome.json` for it to pick up anyway.
871	
872	### Changes Required
873	
874	#### 1. `apps/desktop/.env.example` (new file)
875	
876	**File**: `apps/desktop/.env.example`
877	**Changes**: New file documenting the env surface. Mirrors the schema in `src/env/main.ts` and `src/env/renderer.ts`. Required vars are uncommented; optional vars are commented out with their defaults documented. Phase B's t3-env layer fails fast if a required var is missing — this file is the contract.
878	
879	```
880	# ── Required (main process) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
881	# Clerk publishable key. Used by the main process to decode the Clerk frontend
882	# API domain for the sign-in window CSP. Same value as the monorepo's
883	# NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY in apps/app and apps/www.
884	NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY=pk_test_XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX
885	
886	# ── Optional (main process) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
887	# Main-process API origin. Used by auth-flow.ts for the loopback OAuth callback
888	# base and by index.ts for the CSP connect-src allowlist.
889	# Default: http://localhost:3024
890	# LIGHTFAST_API_URL=http://localhost:3024
891	
892	# Sentry DSN. Leave blank in dev (Sentry init is disabled when buildFlavor=dev).
893	# In CI/release builds, Vite bakes this into the main bundle at package time via
894	# the __SENTRY_DSN__ custom-token define (see vite.main.config.ts).
895	# SENTRY_DSN=
896	
897	# Override Sparkle / Squirrel feed URLs. Leave blank in dev; CI stamps
898	# sparkleFeedUrl into package.json for release builds (desktop-release.yml).
899	# SPARKLE_FEED_URL=
900	# SQUIRREL_FEED_URL=
901	
902	# Override buildFlavor ("dev" | "preview" | "prod") without rebuilding package.json.
903	# BUILD_FLAVOR=dev
904	
905	# Dev-only: enable Chrome DevTools Protocol on the given port (1-65535).
906	# LIGHTFAST_REMOTE_DEBUG_PORT=9222
907	
908	# ── Optional (renderer) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
909	# Renderer-process API origin. Vite only exposes VITE_*-prefixed vars to the
910	# renderer bundle. Mirror LIGHTFAST_API_URL for local dev.
911	# Default: http://localhost:3024
912	# VITE_LIGHTFAST_API_URL=http://localhost:3024
913	
914	# ── Escape hatch ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
915	# Skip t3-env validation entirely (emergency / scripting only).
916	# SKIP_ENV_VALIDATION=1
917	```
918	
919	Note: `apps/desktop/.gitignore` currently ignores `.env`, `.env.development`, and `.env.production` but NOT `.env.example`. Running `git check-ignore apps/desktop/.env.example` should exit 1 (file not ignored). If it's ignored unexpectedly, add `!.env.example` to the desktop-local `.gitignore`.
920	
921	Confirm the existing committed `apps/desktop/.env.development` (which contains only `VITE_LIGHTFAST_API_URL=http://localhost:3024`) stays committed — contributors can copy `.env.example` over it or merge missing fields. After Phase B lands, `.env.development` will need `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` added or `pnpm dev:desktop` will fail fast (by design).
922	
923	#### 2. `apps/desktop/package.json` scripts
924	
925	**File**: `apps/desktop/package.json:7-13`
926	**Changes**: Add `clean` and `sourcemaps:upload`. Do NOT add `lint`/`lint:fix` — root `pnpm check` covers desktop.
927	
928	```json
929	"scripts": {
930	  "clean": "rm -rf out .vite .cache",
931	  "dev": "electron-forge start",
932	  "package": "electron-forge package",
933	  "make": "electron-forge make",
934	  "publish": "electron-forge publish",
935	  "sourcemaps:upload": "node scripts/upload-sourcemaps.mjs",
936	  "typecheck": "tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json && tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.node.json"
937	},
938	```
939	
940	#### 3. Root `package.json:22-23`
941	
942	**File**: `package.json` (root)
943	**Changes**: Fix the misleading `dev:desktop-stack` naming.
944	
945	```json
946	// Before:
947	"dev:desktop": "pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop dev",
948	"dev:desktop-stack": "concurrently --names app,proxy --prefix-colors cyan,magenta 'pnpm dev:full' 'pnpm --filter @lightfast/app proxy:wait'",
949	
950	// After (rename `dev:desktop-stack` → `dev:desktop-api` which is what it actually does):
951	"dev:desktop": "pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop dev",
952	"dev:desktop-api": "concurrently --names app,proxy --prefix-colors cyan,magenta 'pnpm dev:full' 'pnpm --filter @lightfast/app proxy:wait'",
953	```
954	
955	Rationale: the script starts the API-side stack that desktop *talks to*; it does not start desktop itself. `dev:desktop-api` makes intent explicit.
956	
957	#### 4. `.changeset/config.json`
958	
959	**File**: `.changeset/config.json`
960	**Changes**: Add `@lightfast/desktop` to `ignore` (currently `[]`). Use a targeted edit — do not wholesale-rewrite the file, since other fields like `$schema` and `fixed` must stay exactly as-is.
961	
962	```json
963	"ignore": ["@lightfast/desktop"]
964	```
965	
966	Effect: `pnpm changeset` won't prompt for desktop; desktop changes never need a changeset. `verify-changeset.yml:51` allowlist stays untouched — it still rejects desktop-mentioning changesets, which is correct because we've committed to tag-only releases.
967	
968	### Success Criteria
969	
970	#### Automated Verification
971	
972	- [ ] `pnpm check` passes (root ultracite run covers desktop files).
973	- [ ] `pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop clean && pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop typecheck` passes.
974	- [ ] `git check-ignore apps/desktop/.env.example` returns exit 1 (not ignored).
975	
976	#### Manual Verification
977	
978	- [ ] `pnpm changeset` (in interactive mode) does not list `@lightfast/desktop` as a selectable package.
979	- [ ] `pnpm dev:desktop-api` starts the API + proxy stack; `pnpm dev:desktop-stack` no longer exists (errors out cleanly).
980	- [ ] A fresh contributor running `cp apps/desktop/.env.example apps/desktop/.env.development` + populating `VITE_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` can run `pnpm dev:desktop` and sign in.
981	
982	---
983	
984	## Testing Strategy
985	
986	### Typecheck + lint gate
987	
988	Every phase ends with `pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop typecheck` green. Phase F adds `pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop lint` to the gate.
989	
990	### End-to-end release dry run
991	
992	After Phase D lands and Phase A secrets are provisioned:
993	
994	1. Tag `@lightfast/desktop@0.1.0-rc.1`.
995	2. Watch the workflow run green end-to-end.
996	3. Install the arm64 `.dmg` on a personal Mac, sign in, exercise primary + secondary + hud windows.
997	4. Trigger a known error (temporary `throw` in `src/main/index.ts` on a dev tag), confirm Sentry stack trace is symbolicated.
998	5. Publish a second release `@lightfast/desktop@0.1.0-rc.2` with a no-op change; confirm auto-updater picks it up on the running `rc.1` install within 10 seconds of launch.
999	6. Revert the `throw` and delete the pre-release tags.
1000	
1001	### CI gate regression
1002	
1003	Open a throwaway PR with a forced break in `apps/desktop/src/main/index.ts` (e.g. `const x: number = "string"`). Confirm the new `desktop` job fails loudly and blocks merge. Revert before merging.
1004	
1005	## Performance Considerations
1006	
1007	- `desktop-ci.yml` adds ~15 min of `macos-14` runtime on desktop-touching PRs only (native `paths:` filter means GitHub doesn't schedule a runner otherwise). Public repo → free CI minutes, so cost is not a factor; on paid plans, `macos-14` minutes cost ~10× Linux, but the job fires only on relevant PRs. Acceptable.
1008	- Source-map upload adds ~30s to each release arch leg. Acceptable.
1009	- Sentry release creation is a single API call, negligible.
1010	
1011	## Migration Notes
1012	
1013	- Pre-release installs of the unsigned dev build (`pnpm package` output) won't auto-update. Not a concern — nobody is running the dev `.app` outside local testing.
1014	- `LIGHTFAST_DESKTOP_RELEASE_REPO` env var is gone after Phase B. If anyone has it in a local `.env.development`, remove it. No on-disk state affected.
1015

1031	## Improvement Log
1032	
1033	### 2026-04-24 — Adversarial review (`/improve_plan`)
1034	
1035	Four decisions changed after reviewing the plan against the actual codebase. Plan file edited in-place; this log captures what moved and why.
1036	
1037	**1. Sentry DSN no longer flows through `build-info` / `BuildInfoSnapshot`.** Earlier draft of this plan threaded a new `sentryDsn` field through `package.json` → `env.ts` schema → `build-info.ts` → `ipc.ts` IPC contract → `sentry.ts`, and stamped it via `npm pkg set sentryDsn="$SENTRY_DSN"` in CI. Replaced with a single Vite `define` in `vite.main.config.ts` + a one-line change in `sentry.ts` that reads `process.env.SENTRY_DSN` directly. Vite's syntactic replacement bakes the literal DSN into `.vite/build/index-<hash>.js` at package time.
1038	   - **Spike verdict: CONFIRMED** (worktree `agent-a9a9641300bfb70b8`). Baseline build with no env var produced `const dsn = "";` in the bundle. Build with `SENTRY_DSN='https://fake@sentry.io/12345' pnpm package` produced `const dsn = "https://fake@sentry.io/12345";`. `rg "process.env.SENTRY_DSN" apps/desktop/.vite/build/*.js` returned 0 hits in both builds. Typecheck passed on first try.
1039	   - **Net change**: 2 files touched instead of 6; no schema churn; no secret value mutated into `package.json`; no per-step CI plumbing beyond adding `SENTRY_DSN` to the build-job `env:` block (which Phase A secrets work already required).
1040	   - Phase D stamp step correspondingly drops the `npm pkg set sentryDsn` line.
1041	
1042	**2. Desktop CI is a standalone workflow, not a `dorny/paths-filter` job bolted onto `ci.yml`.** The repo has zero prior uses of `dorny/paths-filter`; `verify-changeset.yml:12-14` already uses native workflow-level `on: push: paths:`. Phase E now creates `.github/workflows/desktop-ci.yml` with native path filtering — no `if:` guards on every step, no new third-party action. The dep list also expanded from `packages/app-trpc/**` alone to include `packages/ui/**`, `packages/lib/**`, `pnpm-workspace.yaml`, and the workflow file itself.
1043	
1044	**3. No per-package `lint` script for desktop.** Phase F originally added `"lint": "biome check"` + `"lint:fix"` to `apps/desktop/package.json`. Zero workspace packages in the monorepo define their own lint script today — the convention is root `pnpm check` → `npx ultracite@latest check`, which walks the whole tree. Also, no `biome.json` exists for a per-package `biome check` to pick up. Both scripts removed; Phase F now only adds `clean` and `sourcemaps:upload`.
1045	
1046	**4. `.env.example` now documents both `LIGHTFAST_API_URL` and `VITE_LIGHTFAST_API_URL`.** The earlier version only listed the unprefixed main-process variable, but Vite exposes only `VITE_*`-prefixed vars to the renderer. Both are required for the renderer-side tRPC client to hit localhost in dev. Also fixed `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` → `VITE_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` (the desktop renderer is not Next.js).
1047	
1048	### Additional small fixes from the review
1049	
1050	- Phase B now includes a README update to delete the `LIGHTFAST_DESKTOP_RELEASE_REPO=lightfastai/lightfast` reference at `apps/desktop/README.md:158`, which would otherwise go stale the day Phase B lands.
1051	- Added prerequisites callout for `2026-04-24-coderabbit-pr614-fixes.md` — the port-collision and `will-navigate` fixes on the loopback OAuth flow need to be in before Phase D's end-to-end validation, otherwise a signed release could crash during sign-in.
1052	- Performance Considerations section clarified that `macos-14` minutes cost ~10× Linux on paid plans; acceptable here because the workflow only fires on desktop-touching paths and the repo is public (free minutes).
1053	
1054	---
1055	
1056	### 2026-04-24 — Second adversarial review (env layer overhaul)
1057	
1058	User flagged that the Sentry-DSN-only fix in the first review was a half-measure and asked for a proper env layer inspired by `apps/www/src/env.ts`. Phase B rewritten substantially. The earlier Sentry-DSN spike still holds (DSN does bake via Vite `define`), but the mechanism and surface changed.
1059	
1060	**1. Replaced hand-rolled `parseRuntimeEnv` with t3-env.** Every other workspace in the monorepo uses `@t3-oss/env-core` (non-Next.js) or `@t3-oss/env-nextjs` — desktop was the only outlier with a hand-rolled zod validator. The old `parseRuntimeEnv` silently returned `{}` on validation failure (`apps/desktop/src/shared/env.ts:26-31`), the exact opposite of the "build-fail-fast" behavior that makes `apps/www/next.config.ts` safe. Phase B now introduces `apps/desktop/src/env/main.ts` (main + preload, `isServer: true`) and `apps/desktop/src/env/renderer.ts` (`VITE_*` prefix, `runtimeEnv: import.meta.env`). The old `src/shared/env.ts` is deleted; `buildInfoSchema` (a data contract, not env validation) is rehomed to `src/shared/build-info-schema.ts`.
1061	   - **Spike verdict: CONFIRMED** (worktree `agent-ad5ab5b18d79dea9e`, 2026-04-24). Added `@t3-oss/env-core: catalog:` to desktop, created `src/env/main.ts`, imported it from `src/main/index.ts`, ran Vite main build. `@electron-forge/plugin-vite@7.11.1` bundles everything except electron + `node:*` builtins — t3-env's ESM source is inlined directly into the CJS `bootstrap.js` at build time. No `require("@t3-oss/env-core")` call survives, so no `ERR_REQUIRE_ESM` risk at runtime in the packaged `.app`. `ssr.noExternal` is NOT needed (contra web research on `electron-vite`, which does need it — Electron Forge's Vite plugin has different defaults).
1062	   - **Key finding**: the plan does NOT need a Vite `ssr.noExternal` config addition. If a future Forge version changes bundling behavior, adding `ssr: { noExternal: ['@t3-oss/env-core'] }` to `vite.main.config.ts` is a one-line fallback.
1063	
1064	**2. Sentry DSN bake switched from `process.env.SENTRY_DSN` replacement to a `__SENTRY_DSN__` custom token.** Sentry's official Electron troubleshooting docs explicitly warn: *"Vite recommends using `define` for CONSTANTS only, and not putting `process` or `global` into the options"* — string-replacing `process.*` keys can break Sentry SDK internals. The first-review spike validated only that the literal got baked, not that Sentry runtime behavior survived the replacement. Phase B now uses the Sentry-docs-recommended pattern: `define: { __SENTRY_DSN__: JSON.stringify(process.env.SENTRY_DSN ?? "") }` paired with `declare const __SENTRY_DSN__: string | undefined;` in `src/env/main.ts`, read via `runtimeEnv` with a `process.env.SENTRY_DSN` fallback for dev.
1065	
1066	**3. Expanded the env layer from Sentry-only to the full desktop main-process surface.** The previous Phase B left 4 other env reads on the same fragile pattern (`LIGHTFAST_API_URL` duplicated in `index.ts:45-51` + `auth-flow.ts:10-16`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` with `?? ""` silent fallback, `LIGHTFAST_REMOTE_DEBUG_PORT`, `BUILD_FLAVOR`/`SPARKLE_FEED_URL`/`SQUIRREL_FEED_URL` behind the silent-fail zod wrapper). All now live in `src/env/main.ts` as validated `mainEnv.*` reads. The two duplicated `getApiOrigin()` helpers collapse into a single `mainEnv.LIGHTFAST_API_URL` with a `.default("http://localhost:3024")`.
1067	
1068	**4. Renderer env layer (minimal).** `rendererEnv` validates only `VITE_LIGHTFAST_API_URL` — the single `import.meta.env` access in the renderer tree today (`entry.tsx:7`). Schema slots for future vars (e.g. `VITE_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`) are added in the PRs that introduce the consumers, not pre-emptively in this plan.
1069	
1070	**5. `forge.config.ts` stays on raw `process.env` for CI secrets.** Deliberate non-change: Forge evaluates its config in a plain Node context without Vite's `define`, so the mainEnv module isn't resolvable at that layer. `APPLE_*` / `GITHUB_TOKEN` are CI-only and GitHub Actions failures already surface clearly. Not worth the bootstrap complication.
1071	
1072	**6. `.env.example` rewritten** to match the new schema. Required: `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` (missing → t3-env crashes on startup with a readable error). Everything else is optional with documented defaults. Removed the earlier draft's `VITE_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` slot — it's a renderer var nothing reads yet, and adding unused schema entries is exactly the tech debt this overhaul exists to kill.
1073	
1074	**Success criteria tightened**: new automated checks ensure `parseRuntimeEnv`/`getRuntimeEnv`/`runtimeEnvSchema` identifiers return zero hits post-migration, that raw `process.env.*` for migrated keys only appears in `src/env/main.ts`, and that missing required vars produce t3-env's readable error (not a silent fallback).
1075	
1076	**Research trail**: no canonical Electron + t3-env example exists in public code — the pattern here is assembled from t3-env's docs, Sentry's Electron troubleshooting, and direct verification via the CONFIRMED spike. Desktop becomes the monorepo's reference implementation for non-Next.js t3-env in a three-bundle environment.
1077	
```

1	import { z } from "zod";
2	
3	export const buildFlavorSchema = z.enum(["dev", "preview", "prod"]);
4	export type BuildFlavor = z.infer<typeof buildFlavorSchema>;
5	
6	export const buildInfoSchema = z.object({
7	  name: z.string(),
8	  version: z.string(),
9	  buildFlavor: buildFlavorSchema,
10	  buildNumber: z.string(),
11	  sparkleFeedUrl: z.string(),
12	  sparklePublicKey: z.string(),
13	});
14	
15	export type BuildInfo = z.infer<typeof buildInfoSchema>;
16	
17	export const runtimeEnvSchema = z.object({
18	  SENTRY_DSN: z.string().url().optional(),
19	  SPARKLE_FEED_URL: z.string().url().optional(),
20	  SQUIRREL_FEED_URL: z.string().url().optional(),
21	  BUILD_FLAVOR: buildFlavorSchema.optional(),
22	});
23	
24	export type RuntimeEnv = z.infer<typeof runtimeEnvSchema>;
25	
26	export function parseRuntimeEnv(
27	  env: Record<string, string | undefined>
28	): RuntimeEnv {
29	  const result = runtimeEnvSchema.safeParse(env);
30	  return result.success ? result.data : {};
31	}

1	import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
2	import * as Sentry from "@sentry/electron/main";
3	import { rewriteFramesIntegration } from "@sentry/electron/main";
4	import { app } from "electron";
5	import { getBuildInfo, getRuntimeEnv } from "./build-info";
6	
7	export interface SentryInitOptions {
8	  dsn: string;
9	  enabled: boolean;
10	  environment: string;
11	  release: string;
12	}
13	
14	const SESSION_ID = randomUUID();
15	
16	export function getSentryInitOptions(): SentryInitOptions {
17	  const build = getBuildInfo();
18	  const env = getRuntimeEnv();
19	  const dsn = env.SENTRY_DSN ?? "";
20	  return {
21	    dsn,
22	    release: `${build.name}@${build.version}+${build.buildNumber}`,
23	    environment: build.buildFlavor,
24	    enabled: Boolean(dsn) && build.buildFlavor !== "dev",
25	  };
26	}
27	
28	let initialized = false;
29	
30	export function initSentry(): void {
31	  if (initialized) {
32	    return;
33	  }
34	  const options = getSentryInitOptions();
35	  if (!options.enabled) {
36	    return;
37	  }
38	  const build = getBuildInfo();
39	  Sentry.init({
40	    dsn: options.dsn,
41	    release: options.release,
42	    environment: options.environment,
43	    dist: build.buildNumber,
44	    integrations: [
45	      rewriteFramesIntegration({ root: app.getAppPath(), prefix: "app:///" }),
46	    ],
47	    initialScope: {
48	      tags: {
49	        sessionId: SESSION_ID,
50	        bundle: "electron",
51	        host: "app",
52	      },
53	    },
54	  });
55	  initialized = true;
56	}
57

1	import packageJson from "../../package.json";
2	import {
3	  type BuildInfo,
4	  buildInfoSchema,
5	  parseRuntimeEnv,
6	  type RuntimeEnv,
7	} from "../shared/env";
8	
9	let cachedBuildInfo: BuildInfo | null = null;
10	let cachedRuntimeEnv: RuntimeEnv | null = null;
11	
12	export function getBuildInfo(): BuildInfo {
13	  if (cachedBuildInfo) {
14	    return cachedBuildInfo;
15	  }
16	  const runtime = getRuntimeEnv();
17	  const candidate = {
18	    name: packageJson.name,
19	    version: packageJson.version,
20	    buildFlavor: runtime.BUILD_FLAVOR ?? packageJson.buildFlavor,
21	    buildNumber: packageJson.buildNumber,
22	    sparkleFeedUrl: runtime.SPARKLE_FEED_URL ?? packageJson.sparkleFeedUrl,
23	    sparklePublicKey: packageJson.sparklePublicKey,
24	  };
25	  cachedBuildInfo = buildInfoSchema.parse(candidate);
26	  return cachedBuildInfo;
27	}
28	
29	export function getRuntimeEnv(): RuntimeEnv {
30	  if (cachedRuntimeEnv) {
31	    return cachedRuntimeEnv;
32	  }
33	  cachedRuntimeEnv = parseRuntimeEnv(process.env);
34	  return cachedRuntimeEnv;
35	}
36

2	  app,
3	  BrowserWindow,
4	  ipcMain,
5	  Menu,
6	  nativeTheme,
7	  session,
8	  shell,
9	} from "electron";
10	import contextMenu from "electron-context-menu";
11	import { IpcChannels, type SystemThemeVariant } from "../shared/ipc";
12	import { beginSignIn } from "./auth-flow";
13	import {
14	  getAuthSnapshot,
15	  getToken as getAuthToken,
16	  onAuthChanged,
17	  signOut as signOutAuth,
18	} from "./auth-store";
19	import { getBuildInfo } from "./build-info";
20	import { buildApplicationMenu } from "./menu";
21	import { getSentryInitOptions, initSentry } from "./sentry";
22	import {
23	  getSettings,
24	  onSettingsChanged,
25	  type SettingsSnapshot,
26	  updateSetting,
27	} from "./settings-store";
28	import {
29	  attachLocalShortcuts,
30	  registerGlobalShortcuts,
31	  unregisterGlobalShortcuts,
32	} from "./shortcuts";
33	import { createTray, destroyTray } from "./tray";
34	import { initUpdater, registerUpdaterIpc } from "./updater";
35	import { applyTitleBarOverlayTheme, createWindow } from "./windows/factory";
36	
37	declare const MAIN_WINDOW_VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL: string | undefined;
38	
39	const ALLOWED_EXTERNAL_PROTOCOLS = new Set(["http:", "https:", "mailto:"]);
40	
41	function currentThemeVariant(): SystemThemeVariant {
42	  return nativeTheme.shouldUseDarkColors ? "dark" : "light";
43	}
44	
45	function getApiOriginForCsp(): string {
46	  return (
47	    process.env.LIGHTFAST_API_URL ??
48	    (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production"
49	      ? "https://lightfast.ai"
50	      : "http://localhost:3024")
51	  );
52	}
53	
54	function getClerkFrontendApi(): string | null {
55	  const publishableKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY;
56	  if (!publishableKey) {
57	    return null;
58	  }
59	  const base64Part = publishableKey.split("_")[2];
60	  if (!base64Part) {
61	    return null;
62	  }
63	  try {
64	    const domain = Buffer.from(base64Part, "base64")
65	      .toString("utf-8")
66	      .replace(/\$$/, "");
67	    if (!domain) {
68	      return null;
69	    }
70	    return `https://${domain}`;
71	  } catch {
72	    return null;
73	  }
74	}
75	
76	function buildContentSecurityPolicy(): string {
77	  const apiOrigin = getApiOriginForCsp();
78	  const clerkOrigin = getClerkFrontendApi();
79	  const extraConnect = [apiOrigin, clerkOrigin].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
80	
81	  const devServer = MAIN_WINDOW_VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL;
82	  if (devServer) {
83	    const origin = new URL(devServer).origin;
84	    const wsOrigin = origin.replace(/^http/, "ws");
85	    return [
86	      `default-src 'self' ${origin}`,
87	      `script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' ${origin}`,
88	      `style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' ${origin}`,
89	      `connect-src 'self' ${origin} ${wsOrigin} ${extraConnect}`.trim(),
90	      `img-src 'self' data: blob: ${origin}`,
91	      `font-src 'self' data: ${origin}`,
92	    ].join("; ");
93	  }
94	  return [
95	    "default-src 'self'",
96	    "script-src 'self'",
97	    "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'",
98	    `connect-src 'self' ${extraConnect}`.trim(),
99	    "img-src 'self' data: blob:",
100	    "font-src 'self' data:",
101	  ].join("; ");
102	}
103	
104	function applyContentSecurityPolicy(): void {
105	  const csp = buildContentSecurityPolicy();
106	  session.defaultSession.webRequest.onHeadersReceived((details, callback) => {
107	    callback({
108	      responseHeaders: {
109	        ...details.responseHeaders,
110	        "Content-Security-Policy": [csp],
111	      },
112	    });
113	  });
114	}
115	
116	function hardenContents(contents: Electron.WebContents): void {
117	  contents.setWindowOpenHandler(({ url }) => {
118	    try {
119	      const parsed = new URL(url);
120	      if (ALLOWED_EXTERNAL_PROTOCOLS.has(parsed.protocol)) {
121	        void shell.openExternal(url);
122	      }
123	    } catch {
124	      // ignore malformed urls
125	    }
126	    return { action: "deny" };
127	  });
128	
129	  contents.on("will-navigate", (event, url) => {
130	    const rendererOrigin = MAIN_WINDOW_VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL
131	      ? new URL(MAIN_WINDOW_VITE_DEV_SERVER_URL).origin
132	      : null;
133	    try {
134	      const target = new URL(url);
135	      if (rendererOrigin && target.origin === rendererOrigin) {
136	        return;
137	      }
138	      if (target.protocol === "file:") {
139	        return;
140	      }
141	      event.preventDefault();
142	      if (ALLOWED_EXTERNAL_PROTOCOLS.has(target.protocol)) {
143	        void shell.openExternal(url);
144	      }
145	    } catch {
146	      event.preventDefault();
147	    }
148	  });
149	}
150	
151	function registerIpcHandlers(): void {
152	  ipcMain.on(IpcChannels.getBuildInfoSync, (event) => {
153	    event.returnValue = getBuildInfo();
154	  });
155	
156	  ipcMain.on(IpcChannels.getSentryInitOptionsSync, (event) => {
157	    event.returnValue = getSentryInitOptions();
158	  });
159	
160	  ipcMain.on(IpcChannels.getSettingsSync, (event) => {
161	    event.returnValue = getSettings();
162	  });
163	
164	  ipcMain.handle(IpcChannels.updateSetting, (_event, payload: unknown) => {
165	    if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") {
166	      return getSettings();
167	    }
168	    const { key, value } = payload as {
169	      key: keyof SettingsSnapshot;
170	      value: SettingsSnapshot[keyof SettingsSnapshot];
171	    };
172	    if (!(key in getSettings())) {
173	      return getSettings();
174	    }
175	    return updateSetting(key, value);
176	  });
177	
178	  ipcMain.handle(
179	    IpcChannels.getSystemThemeVariant,
180	    (): SystemThemeVariant => currentThemeVariant()
181	  );
182	
183	  ipcMain.handle(IpcChannels.openExternal, async (_event, url: unknown) => {
184	    if (typeof url !== "string") {
185	      return;
186	    }
187	    try {
188	      const parsed = new URL(url);
189	      if (ALLOWED_EXTERNAL_PROTOCOLS.has(parsed.protocol)) {
190	        await shell.openExternal(url);
191	      }
192	    } catch {
193	      // ignore malformed urls
194	    }
195	  });
196	
197	  ipcMain.on(IpcChannels.rendererError, (_event, payload: unknown) => {
198	    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
199	    console.error("[renderer]", payload);
200	  });
201	
202	  ipcMain.handle(IpcChannels.openWindow, async (_event, kind: unknown) => {
203	    if (kind === "secondary") {
204	      await openSecondaryWindow();
205	    } else if (kind === "hud") {
206	      await openHudWindow();
207	    } else if (kind === "primary") {
208	      await openPrimaryWindow();
209	    }
210	  });
211	
212	  ipcMain.on(IpcChannels.authSnapshotSync, (event) => {
213	    event.returnValue = getAuthSnapshot();
214	  });
215	  ipcMain.handle(IpcChannels.authGetToken, () => getAuthToken());
216	  ipcMain.handle(IpcChannels.authSignIn, () => beginSignIn());
217	  ipcMain.handle(IpcChannels.authSignOut, () => {
218	    signOutAuth();
219	  });
220	}
221	
222	function broadcastThemeUpdates(): void {
223	  nativeTheme.on("updated", () => {
224	    const variant = currentThemeVariant();
225	    for (const win of BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()) {
226	      win.webContents.send(IpcChannels.systemThemeVariantUpdated, variant);
227	      applyTitleBarOverlayTheme(win);
228	    }
229	  });
230	}
231	
232	type Kind = "primary" | "secondary" | "hud";
233	
234	const windowsByKind = new Map<Kind, Set<BrowserWindow>>();
235	
236	function registerWindow(kind: Kind, win: BrowserWindow): void {
237	  const set = windowsByKind.get(kind) ?? new Set<BrowserWindow>();
238	  set.add(win);
239	  windowsByKind.set(kind, set);
240	  win.once("closed", () => {
241	    set.delete(win);
242	  });
243	}
244	
245	async function openKind(kind: Kind): Promise<BrowserWindow> {
246	  const win = await createWindow({ kind, harden: hardenContents });
247	  registerWindow(kind, win);
248	  attachLocalShortcuts(win);
249	  if (!app.isPackaged) {
250	    win.webContents.openDevTools({ mode: "detach" });
251	  }
252	  return win;
253	}
254	
255	export function openPrimaryWindow(): Promise<BrowserWindow> {
256	  return openKind("primary");
257	}
258	
259	export function openSecondaryWindow(): Promise<BrowserWindow> {
260	  return openKind("secondary");
261	}
262	
263	export function openHudWindow(): Promise<BrowserWindow> {
264	  return openKind("hud");
265	}
266	
267	function findWindow(kind: Kind): BrowserWindow | null {
268	  const set = windowsByKind.get(kind);
269	  if (!set) {
270	    return null;
271	  }
272	  for (const win of set) {
273	    if (!win.isDestroyed()) {
274	      return win;
275	    }
276	  }
277	  return null;
278	}
279	
280	function toggleHudWindow(): void {
281	  const existing = findWindow("hud");
282	  if (existing) {
283	    if (existing.isVisible()) {
284	      existing.hide();
285	    } else {
286	      existing.show();
287	      existing.focus();
288	    }
289	  } else {
290	    void openHudWindow();
291	  }
292	}
293	
294	const trayActions = {
295	  showPrimary: () => {
296	    const existing = findWindow("primary");
297	    if (existing) {
298	      existing.show();
299	      existing.focus();
300	    } else {
301	      void openPrimaryWindow();
302	    }
303	  },
304	  toggleHud: toggleHudWindow,
305	};
306	
307	function applySettings(snapshot: SettingsSnapshot): void {
308	  nativeTheme.themeSource = snapshot.themeSource;
309	  if (process.platform !== "linux" && app.isPackaged) {
310	    app.setLoginItemSettings({ openAtLogin: snapshot.launchAtLogin });
311	  }
312	  if (snapshot.showInMenuBar) {
313	    createTray(trayActions);
314	  } else {
315	    destroyTray();
316	  }
317	}
318	
319	function broadcastSettings(snapshot: SettingsSnapshot): void {
320	  for (const win of BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()) {
321	    win.webContents.send(IpcChannels.settingsChanged, snapshot);
322	  }
323	}
324	
325	initSentry();
326	
327	contextMenu({
328	  showInspectElement: !app.isPackaged,
329	  showSaveImageAs: true,
330	  showCopyImage: true,
331	  showSelectAll: true,
332	});
333	
334	app.whenReady().then(() => {
335	  applyContentSecurityPolicy();
336	  session.defaultSession.setPermissionRequestHandler(
337	    (_contents, _permission, callback) => {
338	      callback(false);
339	    }
340	  );
341	
342	  Menu.setApplicationMenu(
343	    buildApplicationMenu({
344	      openSecondary: () => {
345	        void openSecondaryWindow();
346	      },
347	      openHud: () => {
348	        void openHudWindow();
349	      },
350	    })
351	  );
352	
353	  registerIpcHandlers();
354	  registerUpdaterIpc();
355	  broadcastThemeUpdates();
356	  registerGlobalShortcuts({ toggleHud: toggleHudWindow });
357	  applySettings(getSettings());
358	  if (getSettings().checkForUpdatesAutomatically) {
359	    initUpdater();
360	  }
361	  onSettingsChanged((snapshot) => {
362	    applySettings(snapshot);
363	    broadcastSettings(snapshot);
364	  });
365	  void openPrimaryWindow();
366	
367	  onAuthChanged((snapshot) => {
368	    for (const win of BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()) {
369	      win.webContents.send(IpcChannels.authChanged, snapshot);
370	    }
371	  });
372	
373	  app.on("activate", () => {
374	    if (BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length === 0) {
375	      void openPrimaryWindow();
376	    }
377	  });
378	});
379	
380	app.on("will-quit", () => {
381	  unregisterGlobalShortcuts();
382	  destroyTray();
383	});
384	
385	app.on("web-contents-created", (_event, contents) => {
386	  hardenContents(contents);
387	});
388	
389	app.on("window-all-closed", () => {
390	  if (process.platform !== "darwin") {
391	    app.quit();
392	  }
393	});
394

1	import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
2	import { createServer, type Server } from "node:http";
3	import { shell } from "electron";
4	import { setToken } from "./auth-store";
5	
6	const SIGNIN_TIMEOUT_MS = 5 * 60_000;
7	const LOOPBACK_HOST = "127.0.0.1";
8	const CALLBACK_PATH = "/callback";
9	
10	function getApiOrigin(): string {
11	  return (
12	    process.env.LIGHTFAST_API_URL ??
13	    (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production"
14	      ? "https://lightfast.ai"
15	      : "http://localhost:3024")
16	  );
17	}
18	
19	function responsePage(message: string): string {
20	  return `<!doctype html>
21	<html lang="en">
22	  <head>
23	    <meta charset="utf-8" />
24	    <title>Lightfast</title>
25	    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1" />
26	    <style>
27	      html, body { height: 100%; margin: 0; }
28	      body {
29	        display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
30	        font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", system-ui, sans-serif;
31	        background: #0a0a0a; color: #e5e5e5;
32	      }
33	      .card { text-align: center; padding: 2rem; max-width: 28rem; }
34	      h1 { font-size: 1.125rem; font-weight: 600; margin: 0 0 0.5rem; }
35	      p { color: #a3a3a3; margin: 0; font-size: 0.875rem; }
36	    </style>
37	  </head>
38	  <body>
39	    <div class="card">
40	      <h1>${message}</h1>
41	      <p>You can close this tab and return to Lightfast.</p>
42	    </div>
43	  </body>
44	</html>`;
45	}
46	
47	async function startLoopbackServer(): Promise<{
48	  server: Server;
49	  port: number;
50	}> {
51	  const server = createServer();
52	  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
53	    const onError = (error: Error) => {
54	      server.off("listening", onListening);
55	      reject(error);
56	    };
57	    const onListening = () => {
58	      server.off("error", onError);
59	      resolve();
60	    };
61	    server.once("error", onError);
62	    server.once("listening", onListening);
63	    server.listen(0, LOOPBACK_HOST);
64	  });
65	  const address = server.address();
66	  if (!address || typeof address !== "object") {
67	    server.close();
68	    throw new Error("loopback server failed to bind");
69	  }
70	  return { server, port: address.port };
71	}
72	
73	export async function beginSignIn(): Promise<string | null> {
74	  const state = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
75	
76	  let bound: { server: Server; port: number };
77	  try {
78	    bound = await startLoopbackServer();
79	  } catch (error) {
80	    console.error("[auth-flow] loopback bind failed", error);
81	    return null;
82	  }
83	  const { server, port } = bound;
84	  const callbackUrl = `http://${LOOPBACK_HOST}:${port}${CALLBACK_PATH}`;
85	
86	  return new Promise<string | null>((resolve) => {
87	    let settled = false;
88	    const settle = (token: string | null) => {
89	      if (settled) {
90	        return;
91	      }
92	      settled = true;
93	      clearTimeout(timer);
94	      server.close();
95	      if (token) {
96	        setToken(token);
97	      }
98	      resolve(token);
99	    };
100	
101	    const timer = setTimeout(() => settle(null), SIGNIN_TIMEOUT_MS);
102	
103	    server.on("request", (req, res) => {
104	      try {
105	        const url = new URL(req.url ?? "/", `http://${LOOPBACK_HOST}:${port}`);
106	        if (url.pathname !== CALLBACK_PATH) {
107	          res.writeHead(404, { "Content-Type": "text/plain" });
108	          res.end("Not Found");
109	          return;
110	        }
111	        const token = url.searchParams.get("token");
112	        const returned = url.searchParams.get("state");
113	        const ok = !!token && returned === state;
114	        res.writeHead(ok ? 200 : 400, { "Content-Type": "text/html" });
115	        res.end(responsePage(ok ? "Signed in to Lightfast" : "Sign-in failed"));
116	        settle(ok ? token : null);
117	      } catch (error) {
118	        console.error("[auth-flow] loopback handler error", error);
119	        res.writeHead(500, { "Content-Type": "text/plain" });
120	        res.end("Internal Server Error");
121	        settle(null);
122	      }
123	    });
124	
125	    server.on("error", (error) => {
126	      console.error("[auth-flow] loopback server error", error);
127	      settle(null);
128	    });
129	
130	    const signInUrl = new URL("/desktop/auth", getApiOrigin());
131	    signInUrl.searchParams.set("state", state);
132	    signInUrl.searchParams.set("callback", callbackUrl);
133	
134	    console.log(
135	      `[auth-flow] signin url=${signInUrl.toString()} callback=${callbackUrl}`
136	    );
137	
138	    shell.openExternal(signInUrl.toString()).catch((error) => {
139	      console.error("[auth-flow] shell.openExternal failed", error);
140	      settle(null);
141	    });
142	  });
143	}
144

1	import { join } from "node:path";
2	import { app } from "electron";
3	import squirrelStartup from "electron-squirrel-startup";
4	
5	if (squirrelStartup) {
6	  app.quit();
7	}
8	
9	const productName = app.isPackaged ? "Lightfast" : "Lightfast Dev";
10	app.setName(productName);
11	app.setPath("userData", join(app.getPath("appData"), productName));
12	
13	if (!app.isPackaged) {
14	  const raw = process.env.LIGHTFAST_REMOTE_DEBUG_PORT?.trim();
15	  if (raw) {
16	    const port = Number(raw);
17	    if (Number.isInteger(port) && port >= 1 && port <= 65_535) {
18	      app.commandLine.appendSwitch("remote-debugging-port", String(port));
19	      app.commandLine.appendSwitch("remote-debugging-address", "127.0.0.1");
20	      console.log(`[cdp] remote debugging on 127.0.0.1:${port}`);
21	    } else {
22	      console.warn(
23	        `[cdp] ignoring LIGHTFAST_REMOTE_DEBUG_PORT=${raw}; expected integer 1-65535`
24	      );
25	    }
26	  }
27	}
28	
29	if (!app.requestSingleInstanceLock()) {
30	  app.exit(0);
31	}
32	
33	import("./index").catch((error) => {
34	  console.error("Failed to load main module", error);
35	  app.exit(1);
36	});

1	import { app, autoUpdater, BrowserWindow, ipcMain } from "electron";
2	import { IpcChannels } from "../shared/ipc";
3	import { getBuildInfo, getRuntimeEnv } from "./build-info";
4	
5	export interface UpdaterStatus {
6	  message?: string;
7	  progress?: number;
8	  state:
9	    | "idle"
10	    | "checking"
11	    | "available"
12	    | "not-available"
13	    | "downloading"
14	    | "ready"
15	    | "error";
16	}
17	
18	let currentStatus: UpdaterStatus = { state: "idle" };
19	let initialized = false;
20	
21	function broadcastStatus(): void {
22	  for (const win of BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()) {
23	    win.webContents.send(IpcChannels.updaterStatusChanged, currentStatus);
24	  }
25	}
26	
27	function expandFeedUrl(url: string): string {
28	  return url.replace(/\$\{arch\}/g, process.arch);
29	}
30	
31	function resolveFeedUrl(): string | null {
32	  const build = getBuildInfo();
33	  const env = getRuntimeEnv();
34	  if (process.platform === "darwin") {
35	    const raw = env.SPARKLE_FEED_URL ?? (build.sparkleFeedUrl || null);
36	    return raw ? expandFeedUrl(raw) : null;
37	  }
38	  if (process.platform === "win32") {
39	    return env.SQUIRREL_FEED_URL ?? null;
40	  }
41	  return null;
42	}
43	
44	export function registerUpdaterIpc(): void {
45	  ipcMain.handle(IpcChannels.updaterCheck, async () => {
46	    if (!initialized) {
47	      return { ok: false, reason: "updater-disabled" };
48	    }
49	    try {
50	      autoUpdater.checkForUpdates();
51	      return { ok: true };
52	    } catch (error) {
53	      return {
54	        ok: false,
55	        reason: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "unknown",
56	      };
57	    }
58	  });
59	
60	  ipcMain.handle(IpcChannels.updaterInstall, () => {
61	    if (currentStatus.state !== "ready") {
62	      return;
63	    }
64	    autoUpdater.quitAndInstall();
65	  });
66	
67	  ipcMain.on(IpcChannels.updaterStatusSync, (event) => {
68	    event.returnValue = currentStatus;
69	  });
70	}
71	
72	export function initUpdater(): void {
73	  if (initialized) {
74	    return;
75	  }
76	  if (!app.isPackaged) {
77	    return;
78	  }
79	  const build = getBuildInfo();
80	  if (build.buildFlavor === "dev") {
81	    return;
82	  }
83	
84	  const feedUrl = resolveFeedUrl();
85	  if (!feedUrl) {
86	    return;
87	  }
88	
89	  try {
90	    autoUpdater.setFeedURL({ url: feedUrl });
91	  } catch {
92	    currentStatus = { state: "error", message: "Feed URL rejected" };
93	    broadcastStatus();
94	    return;
95	  }
96	
97	  autoUpdater.on("checking-for-update", () => {
98	    currentStatus = { state: "checking" };
99	    broadcastStatus();
100	  });
101	  autoUpdater.on("update-available", () => {
102	    currentStatus = { state: "available" };
103	    broadcastStatus();
104	  });
105	  autoUpdater.on("update-not-available", () => {
106	    currentStatus = { state: "not-available" };
107	    broadcastStatus();
108	  });
109	  autoUpdater.on("update-downloaded", () => {
110	    currentStatus = { state: "ready" };
111	    broadcastStatus();
112	  });
113	  autoUpdater.on("error", (error) => {
114	    currentStatus = { state: "error", message: error.message };
115	    broadcastStatus();
116	  });
117	
118	  initialized = true;
119	
120	  setTimeout(() => {
121	    autoUpdater.checkForUpdates();
122	  }, 10_000);
123	}
124	
```

```
1	# @lightfast/desktop
2	
3	Lightfast desktop app (Electron). Native-looking macOS sidebar via
4	`NSVisualEffectView`, Mica on Windows 11, signed + notarized releases.
5	
6	## Run
7	
8	```bash
9	pnpm -F @lightfast/desktop dev
10	```
11	
12	## Local development
13	
14	The desktop app is a Clerk-authenticated tRPC client for the `apps/app` API.
15	Signed-in requests go through the microfrontends proxy at
16	`http://localhost:3024`, not directly to `apps/app` (4107) — the tRPC route's
17	CORS only whitelists the 3024 origin in dev.
18	
19	### Required env var
20	
21	Create `apps/desktop/.env.development` (gitignored):
22	
23	```
24	VITE_LIGHTFAST_API_URL=http://localhost:3024
25	```
26	
27	Vite injects this into the renderer as `import.meta.env.VITE_LIGHTFAST_API_URL`
28	and `DesktopTRPCProvider` uses it to build the tRPC client.
29	
30	### Clerk JWT template (one-time, per Clerk environment)
31	
32	In the Clerk Dashboard, create a JWT template named exactly `lightfast-desktop`:
33	
34	- **Name**: `lightfast-desktop`
35	- **Expiry**: `86400` seconds (24 hours) — users re-sign-in daily. There is no
36	  silent refresh; when the token expires the renderer's 401 handler clears
37	  local state and the user clicks "Sign in" again.
38	- **Claims**: include `org_id: {{org.id}}` so `orgRouter` procedures work
39	- **Signing**: default (symmetric — the server verifies via `CLERK_SECRET_KEY`)
40	
41	This must be done once in each Clerk environment (dev and prod). The web
42	bridge page (`apps/app/src/app/(app)/(user)/(pending-not-allowed)/desktop/auth/page.tsx`)
43	calls `getToken({ template: "lightfast-desktop" })` — without the template,
44	sign-in will fail with a 400 from Clerk.
45	
46	### Sign-in flow (OS browser + loopback callback)
47	
48	1. User clicks **Sign in with Lightfast** in the desktop app
49	2. Main process starts an ephemeral HTTP listener on
50	   `127.0.0.1:<random-port>` and calls `shell.openExternal(...)` to open the
51	   user's default browser at
52	   `http://localhost:3024/desktop/auth?state=<hex>&callback=http://127.0.0.1:<port>/callback`
53	3. Browser completes Clerk sign-in (instant if already signed in to
54	   lightfast.ai). The bridge page calls `getToken({ template: "lightfast-desktop" })`,
55	   then redirects the tab to the loopback callback with `?token=…&state=…`
56	4. The loopback server validates `state`, persists the token via `safeStorage`,
57	   responds with a "You can close this tab" HTML page, and shuts down
58	5. Main process broadcasts the new auth snapshot to the renderer; UI flips to
59	   signed-in
60	
61	The bridge page only honours `callback` values of the form
62	`http://127.0.0.1:<port>/callback` or `http://localhost:<port>/callback` —
63	anything else is rejected.
64	
65	### Run the stack (two terminals)
66	
67	```bash
68	# Terminal 1 — app + www + platform + microfrontends proxy at 3024
69	pnpm dev:desktop-stack
70	
71	# Terminal 2 — Electron app
72	pnpm dev:desktop
73	```
74	
75	`pnpm dev:full` alone is **not** sufficient — it only boots the Next.js apps
76	at 4107/4101/4112. The microfrontends proxy at 3024 is a separate process
77	launched by `dev:desktop-stack` via `concurrently`.
78	
79	### Inspect the encrypted token store
80	
81	The main process persists the Clerk JWT + refresh cookie via `safeStorage`
82	into a keychain-backed file:
83	
84	```bash
85	# macOS
86	ls ~/Library/Application\ Support/Lightfast/auth.bin
87	```
88	
89	Delete this file to force a fresh sign-in.
90	
91	## The recipe
92	
93	Main process (`src/main/index.ts`):
94	
95	- `vibrancy: "menu"` — macOS `NSVisualEffectView` material
96	- `visualEffectState: "active"` — stay vibrant when unfocused
97	- `titleBarStyle: "hiddenInset"` + `trafficLightPosition: { x: 16, y: 16 }`
98	- `backgroundMaterial: "mica"` — Windows 11 equivalent
99	- `backgroundColor: "#00000000"` — don't paint an opaque background
100	
101	Renderer (`src/renderer/src/styles.css`):
102	
103	- `html, body, #app { background: transparent; }`
104	- Sidebar container: `background: transparent;`
105	- `.titlebar-drag { -webkit-app-region: drag; }` on the top strip;
106	  `-webkit-app-region: no-drag` on buttons.
107	
108	## Release
109	
110	Releases are cut by pushing a tag matching `desktop-v<version>`:
111	
112	```bash
113	git tag desktop-v0.1.0
114	git push origin desktop-v0.1.0
115	```
116	
117	The `Release desktop` workflow then:
118	
119	1. Creates a draft GitHub Release for the tag.
120	2. Builds signed+notarized ZIP and DMG artifacts for both `arm64` and `x64`
121	   via `electron-forge publish` on `macos-14`.
122	3. Generates `latest-mac-<arch>.json` (Squirrel.Mac feed format) and uploads
123	   them to the release. The app's updater points at
124	   `releases/latest/download/latest-mac-${arch}.json`.
125	4. Undrafts the release.
126	
127	### Required GitHub secrets
128	
129	| Secret | Contents |
130	| --- | --- |
131	| `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY` | e.g. `Developer ID Application: Lightfast, Inc. (TEAMID)` |
132	| `APPLE_TEAM_ID` | Apple Developer Team ID |
133	| `APPLE_CERT_BASE64` | `base64 -i cert.p12 \| pbcopy` of the Developer ID `.p12` |
134	| `APPLE_CERT_PASSWORD` | Password used to export the `.p12` |
135	| `KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD` | Any string — used for the ephemeral CI keychain |
136	| `APPLE_API_KEY_CONTENT` | Full contents of the App Store Connect `.p8` file |
137	| `APPLE_API_KEY_ID` | 10-char key ID for the above `.p8` |
138	| `APPLE_API_ISSUER` | App Store Connect issuer UUID |
139	
140	`GITHUB_TOKEN` is provided automatically — no secret needed.
141	
142	### Local dry-run
143	
144	Run the packager locally without publishing:
145	
146	```bash
147	pnpm -F @lightfast/desktop exec electron-forge package \
148	  --arch=arm64 --platform=darwin
149	```
150	
151	Full sign/notarize/publish locally (requires the same env vars the workflow
152	sets):
153	
154	```bash
155	APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY=... APPLE_TEAM_ID=... \
156	APPLE_API_KEY=$HOME/.private_keys/AuthKey_XXX.p8 \
157	APPLE_API_KEY_ID=... APPLE_API_ISSUER=... \
158	LIGHTFAST_DESKTOP_RELEASE_REPO=lightfastai/lightfast \
159	GITHUB_TOKEN=$(gh auth token) \
160	pnpm -F @lightfast/desktop exec electron-forge publish \
161	  --arch=arm64 --platform=darwin
162	```
163	
```

1	import { contextBridge, type IpcRendererEvent, ipcRenderer } from "electron";
2	import type { AcceleratorName } from "../shared/accelerators";
3	import {
4	  type AuthSnapshot,
5	  type BuildInfoSnapshot,
6	  IpcChannels,
7	  type LightfastBridge,
8	  type SentryInitSnapshot,
9	  type SettingsSnapshot,
10	  type SystemThemeVariant,
11	  type UpdaterStatusSnapshot,
12	  type WindowKind,
13	} from "../shared/ipc";
14	
15	const buildInfo = ipcRenderer.sendSync(
16	  IpcChannels.getBuildInfoSync
17	) as BuildInfoSnapshot;
18	const sentryInit = ipcRenderer.sendSync(
19	  IpcChannels.getSentryInitOptionsSync
20	) as SentryInitSnapshot;
21	const updaterStatus = ipcRenderer.sendSync(
22	  IpcChannels.updaterStatusSync
23	) as UpdaterStatusSnapshot;
24	const settings = ipcRenderer.sendSync(
25	  IpcChannels.getSettingsSync
26	) as SettingsSnapshot;
27	const authSnapshot = ipcRenderer.sendSync(
28	  IpcChannels.authSnapshotSync
29	) as AuthSnapshot;
30	
31	const bridge: LightfastBridge = {
32	  auth: {
33	    snapshot: authSnapshot,
34	    getToken: () => ipcRenderer.invoke(IpcChannels.authGetToken),
35	    signIn: () => ipcRenderer.invoke(IpcChannels.authSignIn),
36	    signOut: () => ipcRenderer.invoke(IpcChannels.authSignOut),
37	    onChanged: (listener) => {
38	      const handler = (_event: IpcRendererEvent, snap: AuthSnapshot) =>
39	        listener(snap);
40	      ipcRenderer.on(IpcChannels.authChanged, handler);
41	      return () => ipcRenderer.off(IpcChannels.authChanged, handler);
42	    },
43	  },
44	  buildInfo,
45	  sentryInit,
46	  platform: process.platform,
47	  getSystemThemeVariant: () =>
48	    ipcRenderer.invoke(IpcChannels.getSystemThemeVariant),
49	  onSystemThemeVariantUpdated: (listener) => {
50	    const handler = (_event: unknown, variant: SystemThemeVariant) =>
51	      listener(variant);
52	    ipcRenderer.on(IpcChannels.systemThemeVariantUpdated, handler);
53	    return () =>
54	      ipcRenderer.off(IpcChannels.systemThemeVariantUpdated, handler);
55	  },
56	  onUpdaterStatusChanged: (listener) => {
57	    const handler = (_event: unknown, status: UpdaterStatusSnapshot) =>
58	      listener(status);
59	    ipcRenderer.on(IpcChannels.updaterStatusChanged, handler);
60	    return () => ipcRenderer.off(IpcChannels.updaterStatusChanged, handler);
61	  },
62	  onMenuAction: (listener) => {
63	    const handler = (_event: unknown, action: AcceleratorName) =>
64	      listener(action);
65	    ipcRenderer.on(IpcChannels.menuAction, handler);
66	    return () => ipcRenderer.off(IpcChannels.menuAction, handler);
67	  },
68	  onSettingsChanged: (listener) => {
69	    const handler = (_event: unknown, snapshot: SettingsSnapshot) =>
70	      listener(snapshot);
71	    ipcRenderer.on(IpcChannels.settingsChanged, handler);
72	    return () => ipcRenderer.off(IpcChannels.settingsChanged, handler);
73	  },
74	  openExternal: (url) => ipcRenderer.invoke(IpcChannels.openExternal, url),
75	  openWindow: (kind) => ipcRenderer.invoke(IpcChannels.openWindow, kind),
76	  reportError: (payload) =>
77	    ipcRenderer.send(IpcChannels.rendererError, payload),
78	  settings,
79	  updateSetting: (key, value) =>
80	    ipcRenderer.invoke(IpcChannels.updateSetting, { key, value }),
81	  updater: {
82	    status: updaterStatus,
83	    check: () => ipcRenderer.invoke(IpcChannels.updaterCheck),
84	    install: () => ipcRenderer.invoke(IpcChannels.updaterInstall),
85	  },
86	};
87	
88	contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld("lightfastBridge", bridge);
89	
90	const windowKind: WindowKind =
91	  (process.argv
92	    .find((arg) => arg.startsWith("--window-kind="))
93	    ?.slice("--window-kind=".length) as WindowKind | undefined) ?? "primary";
94	contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld("codexWindowType", windowKind);
95	
```

  "new_string": "import { join } from \"node:path\";\nimport { app } from \"electron\";\nimport squirrelStartup from \"electron-squirrel-startup\";\nimport { mainEnv } from \"../env/main\";\n\nif (squirrelStartup) {\n  app.quit();\n}\n\nconst productName = app.isPackaged ? \"Lightfast\" : \"Lightfast Dev\";\napp.setName(productName);\napp.setPath(\"userData\", join(app.getPath(\"appData\"), productName));\n\nif (!app.isPackaged) {\n  const port = mainEnv.LIGHTFAST_REMOTE_DEBUG_PORT;\n  if (port !== undefined) {\n    app.commandLine.appendSwitch(\"remote-debugging-port\", String(port));\n    app.commandLine.appendSwitch(\"remote-debugging-address\", \"127.0.0.1\");\n    console.log(`[cdp] remote debugging on 127.0.0.1:${port}`);\n  }\n}"
}
