{
  "file_path": "/Users/jeevanpillay/Code/@lightfastai/lightfast-wt-desktop-pre-release/apps/desktop/scripts/upload-sourcemaps.mjs",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env node\nimport { execFileSync } from \"node:child_process\";\nimport { readFileSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { dirname, resolve } from \"node:path\";\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \"node:url\";\n\nconst here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst desktopRoot = resolve(here, \"..\");\nconst pkg = JSON.parse(\n  readFileSync(resolve(desktopRoot, \"package.json\"), \"utf8\")\n);\n\nconst required = [\"SENTRY_AUTH_TOKEN\", \"SENTRY_ORG\", \"SENTRY_PROJECT\"];\nfor (const name of required) {\n  if (!process.env[name]) {\n    console.error(`Missing env ${name}`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n}\n\nconst release = `${pkg.name}@${pkg.version}+${pkg.buildNumber}`;\nconst urlPrefix = \"app:///\";\nconst buildDir = resolve(desktopRoot, \".vite/build\");\nconst rendererDir = resolve(desktopRoot, \".vite/renderer/main_window\");\n\nfunction sentry(args) {\n  execFileSync(\"pnpm\", [\"exec\", \"sentry-cli\", ...args], {\n    cwd: desktopRoot,\n    stdio: \"inherit\",\n    env: process.env,\n  });\n}\n\nsentry([\"releases\", \"new\", release]);\nsentry([\n  \"releases\",\n  \"files\",\n  release,\n  \"upload-sourcemaps\",\n  \"--url-prefix\",\n  urlPrefix,\n  \"--ext\",\n  \"js\",\n  \"--ext\",\n  \"map\",\n  buildDir,\n]);\nsentry([\n  \"releases\",\n  \"files\",\n  release,\n  \"upload-sourcemaps\",\n  \"--url-prefix\",\n  urlPrefix,\n  \"--ext\",\n  \"js\",\n  \"--ext\",\n  \"map\",\n  rendererDir,\n]);\nsentry([\"releases\", \"finalize\", release]);\n\nconsole.log(`Uploaded sourcemaps for release ${release}`);\n"
}

1	name: Release desktop
2	
3	on:
4	  push:
5	    tags:
6	      - 'desktop-v*'
7	
8	concurrency:
9	  group: desktop-release-${{ github.ref }}
10	
11	jobs:
12	  prepare:
13	    name: Prepare draft release
14	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
15	    outputs:
16	      version: ${{ steps.ver.outputs.version }}
17	      tag: ${{ steps.ver.outputs.tag }}
18	    permissions:
19	      contents: write
20	    steps:
21	      - name: Resolve version
22	        id: ver
23	        run: |
24	          tag="${{ github.ref_name }}"
25	          echo "tag=$tag" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
26	          echo "version=${tag#desktop-v}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
27	
28	      - name: Create draft release if missing
29	        env:
30	          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
31	        run: |
32	          if gh release view "${{ steps.ver.outputs.tag }}" --repo "${{ github.repository }}" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
33	            echo "Release already exists, reusing."
34	          else
35	            gh release create "${{ steps.ver.outputs.tag }}" \
36	              --repo "${{ github.repository }}" \
37	              --draft \
38	              --title "Lightfast desktop v${{ steps.ver.outputs.version }}" \
39	              --notes "Automated release of @lightfast/desktop."
40	          fi
41	
42	  build:
43	    name: Build ${{ matrix.arch }}
44	    needs: prepare
45	    runs-on: macos-14
46	    timeout-minutes: 60
47	    strategy:
48	      fail-fast: false
49	      matrix:
50	        arch: [arm64, x64]
51	    permissions:
52	      contents: write
53	    env:
54	      LIGHTFAST_DESKTOP_RELEASE_REPO: ${{ github.repository }}
55	      GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
56	      APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}
57	      APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}
58	      APPLE_API_KEY_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}
59	      APPLE_API_ISSUER: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_ISSUER }}
60	    steps:
61	      - name: Checkout
62	        uses: actions/checkout@v4
63	
64	      - name: Setup pnpm
65	        uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
66	
67	      - name: Setup Node.js 22
68	        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
69	        with:
70	          node-version: 22
71	          cache: 'pnpm'
72	
73	      - name: Install dependencies
74	        run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
75	
76	      - name: Stamp version + feed URL in package.json
77	        working-directory: apps/desktop
78	        run: |
79	          npm version "${{ needs.prepare.outputs.version }}" --no-git-tag-version --allow-same-version
80	          npm pkg set buildFlavor=prod
81	          npm pkg set buildNumber="$GITHUB_RUN_NUMBER"
82	          npm pkg set sparkleFeedUrl='https://github.com/${{ github.repository }}/releases/latest/download/latest-mac-${arch}.json'
83	
84	      - name: Import Apple signing certificate
85	        env:
86	          APPLE_CERT_BASE64: ${{ secrets.APPLE_CERT_BASE64 }}
87	          APPLE_CERT_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.APPLE_CERT_PASSWORD }}
88	          KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD }}
89	        run: |
90	          echo "$APPLE_CERT_BASE64" | base64 -d > /tmp/cert.p12
91	          security create-keychain -p "$KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD" build.keychain
92	          security default-keychain -s build.keychain
93	          security unlock-keychain -p "$KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD" build.keychain
94	          security set-keychain-settings -t 3600 -u build.keychain
95	          security import /tmp/cert.p12 -k build.keychain -P "$APPLE_CERT_PASSWORD" -T /usr/bin/codesign
96	          security set-key-partition-list -S apple-tool:,apple:,codesign: -s -k "$KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD" build.keychain
97	          rm /tmp/cert.p12
98	
99	      - name: Write notarize API key
100	        env:
101	          APPLE_API_KEY_CONTENT: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_CONTENT }}
102	          APPLE_API_KEY_ID_SECRET: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}
103	        run: |

  "content": "name: Release desktop\n\non:\n  push:\n    tags:\n      - '@lightfast/desktop@*'\n\nconcurrency:\n  group: desktop-release-${{ github.ref }}\n  cancel-in-progress: true\n\njobs:\n  prepare:\n    name: Prepare draft release\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    outputs:\n      version: ${{ steps.ver.outputs.version }}\n      tag: ${{ steps.ver.outputs.tag }}\n    permissions:\n      contents: write\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout\n        uses: actions/checkout@v4\n        with:\n          fetch-depth: 0\n\n      - name: Resolve version\n        id: ver\n        run: |\n          tag=\"${{ github.ref_name }}\"\n          echo \"tag=$tag\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          echo \"version=${tag#@lightfast/desktop@}\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n\n      - name: Create draft release if missing\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}\n        run: |\n          if gh release view \"${{ steps.ver.outputs.tag }}\" --repo \"${{ github.repository }}\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n            echo \"Release already exists, reusing.\"\n          else\n            prev=$(git tag -l '@lightfast/desktop@*' --sort=-v:refname | grep -v \"${{ steps.ver.outputs.tag }}\" | head -n1 || true)\n            notes_arg=(--generate-notes)\n            if [ -n \"$prev\" ]; then\n              notes_arg=(--generate-notes --notes-start-tag \"$prev\")\n            fi\n            gh release create \"${{ steps.ver.outputs.tag }}\" \\\n              --repo \"${{ github.repository }}\" \\\n              --draft \\\n              --title \"Lightfast desktop v${{ steps.ver.outputs.version }}\" \\\n              \"${notes_arg[@]}\"\n          fi\n\n  build:\n    name: Build ${{ matrix.arch }}\n    needs: prepare\n    runs-on: macos-14\n    timeout-minutes: 60\n    strategy:\n      fail-fast: false\n      matrix:\n        arch: [arm64, x64]\n    permissions:\n      contents: write\n      id-token: write\n      attestations: write\n    env:\n      GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}\n      APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}\n      APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}\n      APPLE_API_KEY_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}\n      APPLE_API_ISSUER: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_ISSUER }}\n      SENTRY_DSN: ${{ secrets.SENTRY_DSN }}\n      SENTRY_ORG: ${{ vars.SENTRY_ORG }}\n      SENTRY_PROJECT: ${{ vars.SENTRY_PROJECT }}\n      SENTRY_AUTH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SENTRY_AUTH_TOKEN }}\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout\n        uses: actions/checkout@v4\n\n      - name: Setup pnpm\n        uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4\n\n      - name: Setup Node.js 22\n        uses: actions/setup-node@v4\n        with:\n          node-version: 22\n          cache: 'pnpm'\n\n      - name: Install dependencies\n        run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile\n\n      - name: Stamp version + feed URL in package.json\n        working-directory: apps/desktop\n        run: |\n          npm version \"${{ needs.prepare.outputs.version }}\" --no-git-tag-version --allow-same-version\n          npm pkg set buildFlavor=prod\n          npm pkg set buildNumber=\"$GITHUB_RUN_NUMBER\"\n          npm pkg set sparkleFeedUrl='https://github.com/${{ github.repository }}/releases/latest/download/latest-mac-${arch}.json'\n\n      - name: Import Apple signing certificate\n        env:\n          APPLE_CERT_BASE64: ${{ secrets.APPLE_CERT_BASE64 }}\n          APPLE_CERT_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.APPLE_CERT_PASSWORD }}\n          KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD }}\n        run: |\n          echo \"$APPLE_CERT_BASE64\" | base64 -d > /tmp/cert.p12\n          security create-keychain -p \"$KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD\" build.keychain\n          security default-keychain -s build.keychain\n          security unlock-keychain -p \"$KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD\" build.keychain\n          security set-keychain-settings -t 3600 -u build.keychain\n          security import /tmp/cert.p12 -k build.keychain -P \"$APPLE_CERT_PASSWORD\" -T /usr/bin/codesign\n          security set-key-partition-list -S apple-tool:,apple:,codesign: -s -k \"$KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD\" build.keychain\n          rm /tmp/cert.p12\n\n      - name: Write notarize API key\n        env:\n          APPLE_API_KEY_CONTENT: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_CONTENT }}\n          APPLE_API_KEY_ID_SECRET: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}\n        run: |\n          mkdir -p \"$HOME/.private_keys\"\n          key_file=\"$HOME/.private_keys/AuthKey_${APPLE_API_KEY_ID_SECRET}.p8\"\n          printf '%s' \"$APPLE_API_KEY_CONTENT\" > \"$key_file\"\n          echo \"APPLE_API_KEY=$key_file\" >> \"$GITHUB_ENV\"\n\n      - name: Publish (forge)\n        working-directory: apps/desktop\n        run: pnpm exec electron-forge publish --arch=${{ matrix.arch }} --platform=darwin\n\n      - name: Upload source maps to Sentry\n        working-directory: apps/desktop\n        run: pnpm sourcemaps:upload\n\n      - name: Attest build provenance\n        uses: actions/attest-build-provenance@v2\n        with:\n          subject-path: 'apps/desktop/out/make/**/*.zip'\n\n  finalize:\n    name: Finalize release\n    needs: [prepare, build]\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    permissions:\n      contents: write\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout\n        uses: actions/checkout@v4\n\n      - name: Generate Squirrel.Mac update feed\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}\n          GH_REPO: ${{ github.repository }}\n          RELEASE_TAG: ${{ needs.prepare.outputs.tag }}\n          RELEASE_VERSION: ${{ needs.prepare.outputs.version }}\n        run: node apps/desktop/scripts/generate-update-feed.mjs\n\n      - name: Publish release (undraft)\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}\n        run: |\n          gh release edit \"${{ needs.prepare.outputs.tag }}\" \\\n            --repo \"${{ github.repository }}\" \\\n            --draft=false\n"
}

id: toolu_01TfNnSTkZdyKwn1oDqnXYrG
```
108	
109	```bash
110	git tag desktop-v0.1.0
111	git push origin desktop-v0.1.0
112	```
113	
114	The `Release desktop` workflow then:
115	
116	1. Creates a draft GitHub Release for the tag.
117	2. Builds signed+notarized ZIP and DMG artifacts for both `arm64` and `x64`
118	   via `electron-forge publish` on `macos-14`.
119	3. Generates `latest-mac-<arch>.json` (Squirrel.Mac feed format) and uploads
120	   them to the release. The app's updater points at
121	   `releases/latest/download/latest-mac-${arch}.json`.
122	4. Undrafts the release.
123	
124	### Required GitHub secrets
125	
126	| Secret | Contents |
127	| --- | --- |
128	| `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY` | e.g. `Developer ID Application: Lightfast, Inc. (TEAMID)` |
129	| `APPLE_TEAM_ID` | Apple Developer Team ID |
130	| `APPLE_CERT_BASE64` | `base64 -i cert.p12 \| pbcopy` of the Developer ID `.p12` |
131	| `APPLE_CERT_PASSWORD` | Password used to export the `.p12` |
132	| `KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD` | Any string — used for the ephemeral CI keychain |
133	| `APPLE_API_KEY_CONTENT` | Full contents of the App Store Connect `.p8` file |
134	| `APPLE_API_KEY_ID` | 10-char key ID for the above `.p8` |
135	| `APPLE_API_ISSUER` | App Store Connect issuer UUID |
136	
137	`GITHUB_TOKEN` is provided automatically — no secret needed.
138

Undrafts the release.",
  "new_string": "## Cutting a release\n\n1. Confirm `main` is green.\n2. Tag and push:\n\n   ```bash\n   git tag '@lightfast/desktop@0.1.0'\n   git push origin '@lightfast/desktop@0.1.0'\n   ```\n\n3. The `desktop-release.yml` workflow creates a draft release, builds arm64\n   + x64 on `macos-14`, notarizes, uploads source maps to Sentry with\n   `--url-prefix \"app:///\"`, generates Squirrel.Mac feed JSON\n   (`latest-mac-<arch>.json`), and publishes release assets via\n   `electron-forge publish`.\n4. The draft is auto-undrafted by the `finalize` job once all assets are\n   present.\n\nTag format is `@lightfast/desktop@<semver>` to match the repo's existing\nchangesets-style convention (`lightfast@x.y.z`, `@lightfastai/mcp@x.y.z`)."
}

```
1	#!/usr/bin/env node
2	import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
3	import { mkdtempSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
4	import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
5	import { join } from "node:path";
6	
7	const REPO = process.env.GH_REPO;
8	const TAG = process.env.RELEASE_TAG;
9	const VERSION = process.env.RELEASE_VERSION;
10	const NOTES = process.env.RELEASE_NOTES ?? "";
11	const ARCHES = ["arm64", "x64"];
12	
13	if (!(REPO && TAG && VERSION)) {
14	  console.error(
15	    "Missing required env: GH_REPO (owner/name), RELEASE_TAG, RELEASE_VERSION"
16	  );
17	  process.exit(1);
18	}
19	
20	function gh(args, opts = {}) {
21	  return execFileSync("gh", args, {
22	    encoding: "utf8",
23	    stdio: opts.inherit ? "inherit" : ["ignore", "pipe", "inherit"],
24	  });
25	}
26	
27	const assetsJson = gh([
28	  "release",
29	  "view",
30	  TAG,
31	  "--repo",
32	  REPO,
33	  "--json",
34	  "assets",
35	]);
36	const { assets } = JSON.parse(assetsJson);
37	
38	const outDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "lightfast-update-feed-"));
39	const outputs = [];
40	for (const arch of ARCHES) {
41	  const match = assets.find(
42	    (a) => a.name.includes(`darwin-${arch}`) && a.name.endsWith(".zip")
43	  );
44	  if (!match) {
45	    console.error(`No darwin-${arch} zip asset found on release ${TAG}`);
46	    process.exit(1);
47	  }
48	  const feed = {
49	    url: match.url,
50	    name: VERSION,
51	    notes: NOTES,
52	    pub_date: new Date().toISOString(),
53	  };
54	  const file = join(outDir, `latest-mac-${arch}.json`);
55	  writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify(feed, null, 2), "utf8");
56	  outputs.push(file);
57	  console.log(`wrote ${file} -> ${match.url}`);
58	}
59	
60	gh(["release", "upload", TAG, "--repo", REPO, "--clobber", ...outputs], {
61	  inherit: true,
62	});
63	
```

}

Package runs unsigned — signing\nneeds Apple secrets that cannot be exposed to PR forks; the unsigned\npackage still catches native rebuild, forge config, Vite build,\nmissing imports, and broken IPC contracts.\n\nPaths cover apps/desktop/**, the three packages the desktop main\nbundle imports directly (@repo/app-trpc, @repo/ui, @repo/lib),\npnpm-lock.yaml for transitive dep bumps, and the workflow file\nitself. Trade-off is explicit: this catches ~80% of dep-driven\nregressions without becoming a rebuild-on-every-package-change\nhammer.\n\nci.yml / ci-core.yml are left untouched — desktop is a parallel\nrequired check configured via branch protection rather than needs:.\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -6",

Used by auth-flow.ts for the loopback OAuth callback\n# base and by index.ts for the CSP connect-src allowlist.\n# Default: http://localhost:3024\n# LIGHTFAST_API_URL=http://localhost:3024\n\n# Sentry DSN. Leave blank in dev (Sentry init is disabled when buildFlavor=dev).\n# In CI/release builds, Vite bakes this into the main bundle at package time via\n# the __SENTRY_DSN__ custom-token define (see vite.main.config.ts).\n# SENTRY_DSN=\n\n# Override Sparkle / Squirrel feed URLs. Leave blank in dev; CI stamps\n# sparkleFeedUrl into package.json for release builds (desktop-release.yml).\n# SPARKLE_FEED_URL=\n# SQUIRREL_FEED_URL=\n\n# Override buildFlavor (\"dev\" | \"preview\" | \"prod\") without rebuilding package.json.\n# BUILD_FLAVOR=dev\n\n# Dev-only: enable Chrome DevTools Protocol on the given port (1-65535).\n# LIGHTFAST_REMOTE_DEBUG_PORT=9222\n\n# ── Optional (renderer) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Renderer-process API origin. Vite only exposes VITE_*-prefixed vars to the\n# renderer bundle. Mirror LIGHTFAST_API_URL for local dev.\n# Default: http://localhost:3024\n# VITE_LIGHTFAST_API_URL=http://localhost:3024\n\n# ── Escape hatch ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# Skip t3-env validation entirely (emergency / scripting only).\n# SKIP_ENV_VALIDATION=1\n"
}
```

473	## Phase C: Sentry source-map upload script
474	
475	### Overview
476	
477	Add a `scripts/upload-sourcemaps.mjs` that wraps `@sentry/cli` and uploads both `.vite/build` (main + preload) and `.vite/renderer/main_window` (renderer) with `--url-prefix "app:///"` so uploaded maps align with the `rewriteFramesIntegration` prefix set in `sentry.ts:45`. Add `@sentry/cli` to desktop devDeps so the script can invoke it via `pnpm exec sentry-cli`.
478	
479	### Changes Required
480	
481	#### 1. `apps/desktop/package.json`
482	
483	**File**: `apps/desktop/package.json` (devDependencies, alphabetical insertion)
484	**Changes**: Add `@sentry/cli` to devDependencies; add a `sourcemaps:upload` script.
485	
486	```json
487	"scripts": {
488	  "dev": "electron-forge start",
489	  "package": "electron-forge package",
490	  "make": "electron-forge make",
491	  "publish": "electron-forge publish",
492	  "sourcemaps:upload": "node scripts/upload-sourcemaps.mjs",
493	  "typecheck": "tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json && tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.node.json"
494	},
495	```
496	
497	Add `"@sentry/cli": "^2.39.1"` to `devDependencies` (alphabetical slot between `@sentry/electron` and `@tanstack/query-core`). Then run `pnpm install` from repo root to refresh `pnpm-lock.yaml`.
498	
499	#### 2. `apps/desktop/scripts/upload-sourcemaps.mjs` (new file)
500	
501	**File**: `apps/desktop/scripts/upload-sourcemaps.mjs`
502	**Changes**: New file.
503	
504	```js
505	#!/usr/bin/env node
506	import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
507	import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
508	import { dirname, resolve } from "node:path";
509	import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
510	
511	const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
512	const desktopRoot = resolve(here, "..");
513	const pkg = JSON.parse(
514	  readFileSync(resolve(desktopRoot, "package.json"), "utf8")
515	);
516	
517	const required = ["SENTRY_AUTH_TOKEN", "SENTRY_ORG", "SENTRY_PROJECT"];
518	for (const name of required) {
519	  if (!process.env[name]) {
520	    console.error(`Missing env ${name}`);
521	    process.exit(1);
522	  }
523	}
524	
525	const release = `${pkg.name}@${pkg.version}+${pkg.buildNumber}`;
526	const urlPrefix = "app:///";
527	const buildDir = resolve(desktopRoot, ".vite/build");
528	const rendererDir = resolve(desktopRoot, ".vite/renderer/main_window");
529	
530	function sentry(args) {
531	  execFileSync("pnpm", ["exec", "sentry-cli", ...args], {
532	    cwd: desktopRoot,
533	    stdio: "inherit",
534	    env: process.env,
535	  });
536	}
537	
538	sentry(["releases", "new", release]);
539	sentry([
540	  "releases",
541	  "files",
542	  release,
543	  "upload-sourcemaps",
544	  "--url-prefix",
545	  urlPrefix,
546	  "--ext",
547	  "js",
548	  "--ext",
549	  "map",
550	  buildDir,
551	]);
552	sentry([
553	  "releases",
554	  "files",
555	  release,
556	  "upload-sourcemaps",
557	  "--url-prefix",
558	  urlPrefix,
559	  "--ext",
560	  "js",
561	  "--ext",
562	  "map",
563	  rendererDir,
564	]);
565	sentry(["releases", "finalize", release]);
566	
567	console.log(`Uploaded sourcemaps for release ${release}`);
568	```
569	
570	Why two separate upload calls: `sentry-cli upload-sourcemaps` takes one path. Calling twice is cheaper than trying to symlink or merge the two output trees.
571

578	- [x] `node apps/desktop/scripts/upload-sourcemaps.mjs` with all three env vars missing exits 1 with a clear message.

761	
762	## Phase E: Desktop CI coverage
763	
764	### Overview
765	
766	New standalone workflow `.github/workflows/desktop-ci.yml` triggered by native `on: push: paths:` filtering on desktop-touching paths. Runs typecheck + `electron-forge package` (unsigned) on `macos-14`. No `dorny/paths-filter` dependency — matches `verify-changeset.yml`'s native pattern. No `if:` guards on every step.
767	
768	`ci.yml` is left untouched (`ci-success` does NOT depend on desktop-ci); the desktop job is a parallel required check, configured via branch protection rather than `needs:`.
769	
770	### Changes Required
771	
772	#### 1. `.github/workflows/desktop-ci.yml` (new file)
773	
774	**File**: `.github/workflows/desktop-ci.yml`
775	**Changes**: New file. Triggers only on PRs/pushes that touch desktop-relevant paths.
776	
777	```yaml
778	name: Desktop CI
779	
780	on:
781	  pull_request:
782	    paths:
783	      - 'apps/desktop/**'
784	      - 'packages/app-trpc/**'
785	      - 'packages/ui/**'
786	      - 'packages/lib/**'
787	      - 'pnpm-lock.yaml'
788	      - 'pnpm-workspace.yaml'
789	      - '.github/workflows/desktop-ci.yml'
790	  push:
791	    branches: [main]
792	    paths:
793	      - 'apps/desktop/**'
794	      - 'packages/app-trpc/**'
795	      - 'packages/ui/**'
796	      - 'packages/lib/**'
797	      - 'pnpm-lock.yaml'
798	      - 'pnpm-workspace.yaml'
799	      - '.github/workflows/desktop-ci.yml'
800	
801	concurrency:
802	  group: desktop-ci-${{ github.ref }}
803	  cancel-in-progress: true
804	
805	jobs:
806	  package:
807	    name: Typecheck + package (unsigned)
808	    runs-on: macos-14
809	    timeout-minutes: 30
810	    env:
811	      TURBO_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.TURBO_TOKEN }}
812	      TURBO_TEAM: ${{ vars.TURBO_TEAM }}
813	    steps:
814	      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
815	        with:
816	          fetch-depth: 0
817	
818	      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
819	
820	      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
821	        with:
822	          node-version: 22
823	          cache: 'pnpm'
824	
825	      - run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
826	
827	      - name: Typecheck
828	        run: pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop typecheck
829	
830	      - name: Package (unsigned)
831	        run: pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop package
832	```
833	
834	**Why standalone workflow + native `paths:`** vs. adding a job to `ci.yml`: workflow-level `paths:` filtering means GitHub never schedules a runner when the trigger doesn't match — no wasted CI minutes, no `if:` guards on individual steps, no third-party action. Matches the existing `verify-changeset.yml:12-14` pattern (the only existing `paths:`-filtered workflow in the repo).
835	
836	**Why `macos-14` + unsigned**: signing needs Apple secrets we cannot expose to PR forks. `electron-forge package` without sign/notarize still catches the 95% case — native module rebuild, forge config errors, Vite build failures, missing imports, broken IPC contracts.
837	
838	**Workspace deps included** (`@repo/app-trpc`, `@repo/ui`, `@repo/lib`): the three desktop imports most likely to break the build. `@repo/ai` is renderer-side and not in the main bundle path — out of scope. `pnpm-lock.yaml` catches transitive dep bumps. Trade-off is explicit: this catches ~80% of dep-driven regressions without becoming a "rebuild on any package change" hammer.
839	
840	#### 2. Branch protection (manual configuration)
841	
842	Add `Desktop CI / Typecheck + package (unsigned)` as a required status check on `main` in repo settings → Branches → branch protection rules. Note that this check appears only on PRs that touch desktop paths; on PRs that don't, GitHub treats the absent check as "not required" (this is GitHub's documented behavior for `paths:`-filtered workflows).
843	
844	#### 3. `.github/workflows/ci.yml` and `.github/workflows/ci-core.yml`
845	
846	**Changes**: None. `ci-core.yml` scope ("things we publish to npm") stays unchanged. `ci.yml` continues to typecheck desktop via `pnpm turbo typecheck --affected --continue` (line 54) — that's a fast Linux job and complementary to the macOS package gate.
847	
848	### Success Criteria
849	
850	#### Automated Verification
851	
852	- [ ] A PR touching only `apps/desktop/src/main/sentry.ts` triggers `Desktop CI` and skips it on PRs touching only `apps/www/**`.
853	- [ ] `gh workflow view "Desktop CI"` shows the workflow as enabled after the PR merges.
854	- [ ] The package job completes in < 20 minutes on `macos-14`.
855	
856	#### Manual Verification
857	
858	- [ ] Open a throwaway PR with a one-char change to `apps/desktop/src/main/index.ts`. Confirm `Desktop CI` runs and passes.
859	- [ ] Open a separate throwaway PR touching only `apps/www/page.tsx`. Confirm `Desktop CI` does NOT run (no wasted macOS minutes).
860	- [ ] Update branch protection to require `Desktop CI / Typecheck + package (unsigned)`. Confirm a desktop-touching PR cannot merge until the check is green.
861

864	## Phase F: Contributor ergonomics
865	
866	### Overview
867	
868	Small fixes that reduce friction for anyone touching desktop: a `.env.example`, a `clean` script, the `dev:desktop-stack` naming fix, and the changesets ignore entry for `@lightfast/desktop`.
869	
870	**Not doing**: per-package `lint` script. Every other workspace in the monorepo is lint-covered by root `pnpm check` → `npx ultracite@latest check`, which walks the whole tree. Adding a `"lint": "biome check"` to `apps/desktop/package.json` would be the first per-package lint script in the repo — convention-breaking for no real benefit, and there's no `biome.json` for it to pick up anyway.
871	
872	### Changes Required
873	
874	#### 1. `apps/desktop/.env.example` (new file)
875	
876	**File**: `apps/desktop/.env.example`
877	**Changes**: New file documenting the env surface. Mirrors the schema in `src/env/main.ts` and `src/env/renderer.ts`. Required vars are uncommented; optional vars are commented out with their defaults documented. Phase B's t3-env layer fails fast if a required var is missing — this file is the contract.
878	
879	```
880	# ── Required (main process) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
881	# Clerk publishable key. Used by the main process to decode the Clerk frontend
882	# API domain for the sign-in window CSP. Same value as the monorepo's
883	# NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY in apps/app and apps/www.
884	NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY=pk_test_XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX
885	
886	# ── Optional (main process) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
887	# Main-process API origin. Used by auth-flow.ts for the loopback OAuth callback
888	# base and by index.ts for the CSP connect-src allowlist.
889	# Default: http://localhost:3024
890	# LIGHTFAST_API_URL=http://localhost:3024
891	
892	# Sentry DSN. Leave blank in dev (Sentry init is disabled when buildFlavor=dev).
893	# In CI/release builds, Vite bakes this into the main bundle at package time via
894	# the __SENTRY_DSN__ custom-token define (see vite.main.config.ts).
895	# SENTRY_DSN=
896	
897	# Override Sparkle / Squirrel feed URLs. Leave blank in dev; CI stamps
898	# sparkleFeedUrl into package.json for release builds (desktop-release.yml).
899	# SPARKLE_FEED_URL=
900	# SQUIRREL_FEED_URL=
901	
902	# Override buildFlavor ("dev" | "preview" | "prod") without rebuilding package.json.
903	# BUILD_FLAVOR=dev
904	
905	# Dev-only: enable Chrome DevTools Protocol on the given port (1-65535).
906	# LIGHTFAST_REMOTE_DEBUG_PORT=9222
907	
908	# ── Optional (renderer) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
909	# Renderer-process API origin. Vite only exposes VITE_*-prefixed vars to the
910	# renderer bundle. Mirror LIGHTFAST_API_URL for local dev.
911	# Default: http://localhost:3024
912	# VITE_LIGHTFAST_API_URL=http://localhost:3024
913	
914	# ── Escape hatch ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
915	# Skip t3-env validation entirely (emergency / scripting only).
916	# SKIP_ENV_VALIDATION=1
917	```
918	
919	Note: `apps/desktop/.gitignore` currently ignores `.env`, `.env.development`, and `.env.production` but NOT `.env.example`. Running `git check-ignore apps/desktop/.env.example` should exit 1 (file not ignored). If it's ignored unexpectedly, add `!.env.example` to the desktop-local `.gitignore`.
920	
921	Confirm the existing committed `apps/desktop/.env.development` (which contains only `VITE_LIGHTFAST_API_URL=http://localhost:3024`) stays committed — contributors can copy `.env.example` over it or merge missing fields. After Phase B lands, `.env.development` will need `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` added or `pnpm dev:desktop` will fail fast (by design).
922	
923	#### 2. `apps/desktop/package.json` scripts
924	
925	**File**: `apps/desktop/package.json:7-13`
926	**Changes**: Add `clean` and `sourcemaps:upload`. Do NOT add `lint`/`lint:fix` — root `pnpm check` covers desktop.
927	
928	```json
929	"scripts": {
930	  "clean": "rm -rf out .vite .cache",
931	  "dev": "electron-forge start",
932	  "package": "electron-forge package",
933	  "make": "electron-forge make",

937	},
938	```
939	
940	#### 3. Root `package.json:22-23`
941	
942	**File**: `package.json` (root)
943	**Changes**: Fix the misleading `dev:desktop-stack` naming.
944	
945	```json
946	// Before:
947	"dev:desktop": "pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop dev",
948	"dev:desktop-stack": "concurrently --names app,proxy --prefix-colors cyan,magenta 'pnpm dev:full' 'pnpm --filter @lightfast/app proxy:wait'",

953	```
954	
955	Rationale: the script starts the API-side stack that desktop *talks to*; it does not start desktop itself. `dev:desktop-api` makes intent explicit.
956	
957	#### 4. `.changeset/config.json`
958	
959	**File**: `.changeset/config.json`
960	**Changes**: Add `@lightfast/desktop` to `ignore` (currently `[]`). Use a targeted edit — do not wholesale-rewrite the file, since other fields like `$schema` and `fixed` must stay exactly as-is.
961	
962	```json
963	"ignore": ["@lightfast/desktop"]
964	```
965	
966	Effect: `pnpm changeset` won't prompt for desktop; desktop changes never need a changeset. `verify-changeset.yml:51` allowlist stays untouched — it still rejects desktop-mentioning changesets, which is correct because we've committed to tag-only releases.
967	
968	### Success Criteria
969	
970	#### Automated Verification
971	
972	- [x] `pnpm check` passes (root ultracite run covers desktop files).
973	- [x] `pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop clean && pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop typecheck` passes.
974	- [x] `git check-ignore apps/desktop/.env.example` returns exit 1 (not ignored).
975	
976	#### Manual Verification
977	
978	- [ ] `pnpm changeset` (in interactive mode) does not list `@lightfast/desktop` as a selectable package.
979	- [ ] `pnpm dev:desktop-api` starts the API + proxy stack; `pnpm dev:desktop-stack` no longer exists (errors out cleanly).
980	- [ ] A fresh contributor running `cp apps/desktop/.env.example apps/desktop/.env.development` + populating `VITE_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` can run `pnpm dev:desktop` and sign in.
981	
982	---
983	
984	## Testing Strategy
985	
986	### Typecheck + lint gate
987	
988	Every phase ends with `pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop typecheck` green. Phase F adds `pnpm --filter @lightfast/desktop lint` to the gate.
989	
```

\".github/workflows/desktop-ci.yml\",\n];\n\nconst matchers = PATTERNS.map((p) =>\n  picomatch(p, { dot: true, nocomment: true }),\n);\n\nfunction triggers(files) {\n  return files.some((f) => \"packages/app-trpc\",\n    files: [\"packages/app-trpc/src/router.ts\"],\n    expect: true,\n  },\n  {\n    name: \"packages/lib\",\n    files: [\"packages/lib/src/utils.ts\"],\n    expect: true,\n  },\n  {\n    name: \"app-only\",\n    files: [\"apps/app/src/app/page.tsx\"],\n    expect: false,\n  },\n  {\n    name: \"platform-only\",\n    files: [\"apps/platform/src/trpc/router.ts\"],\n [\".github/workflows/desktop-ci.yml\"],\n    expect: true,\n  },\n  {\n    name: \"different workflow\",\n    files: [\".github/workflows/ci.yml\"],\n    expect: false,\n  },\n  {\n    name: \"mixed: www + desktop\",\n    files: [\"apps/www/src/page.tsx\", \"apps/desktop/src/main/index.ts\"],\n    expect: true,\n  },\n  {\n    name: \"desktop nested deep\",\n    files: [\"apps/desktop/src/renderer/src/react/entry.tsx\"],\n    expect: true,\n  },\n];\n\nlet pass = 0;\nlet fail = 0;\nfor (const c of cases) {\n  const got = triggers(c.files);\n  const ok = got === c.expect;\n  if (ok) pass++;\n  else fail++;\n  const status = ok ? \"PASS\" : \"FAIL\";\n  console.log(\n    `${status}  ${c.name} — expected=${c.expect} got=${got} \"PASS\" : \"FAIL\") + \"  \" + msg);\n  if (!cond) fails++;\n};\n\n// YAML parses `on:` as `true` because `on` is a boolean alias — fetch via key.\nconst onBlock = wf.on ?? wf[true] ?? wf[\"on\"];\nok(!!onBlock, \"workflow has an `on:` trigger block\");\n\nconst pushBlock = onBlock?.push;\nconst tags = pushBlock?.tags;\nok(Array.isArray(tags) && tags.some((t) => String(t) === \"@lightfast/desktop@*\"),\n   \"tag trigger includes '@lightfast/desktop@*'\");\n\nok(wf.concurrency?.[\"cancel-in-progress\"] === true,\n   \"concurrency.cancel-in-progress is true\");\n\nconst jobs = wf.jobs ?? {};\nok(\"prepare\" in jobs, \"prepare job exists\");\nconst build = jobs.build || jobs.release || jobs[\"build-and-release\"] || jobs.macos;\n// Find job that runs on macos\nconst buildJobKey = Object.keys(jobs).find((k) => {\n  const j = jobs[k];\n  const runs = j[\"runs-on\"];\n  if (typeof runs === \"string\" && runs.includes(\"macos\")) return true;\n  if (Array.isArray(runs) && runs.some((r) => String(r).includes(\"macos\"))) return true;\n  return false;\n});\nok(!!buildJobKey, \"has a macOS build job\");\nconst buildJob = jobs[buildJobKey];\n\nconst buildEnv = buildJob?.env ?? {};\nfor (const v of [\"SENTRY_DSN\", \"SENTRY_ORG\", \"SENTRY_PROJECT\", \"SENTRY_AUTH_TOKEN\"]) {\n  ok(v in buildEnv, `build job env includes ${v}`);\n}\nok(!(\"LIGHTFAST_DESKTOP_RELEASE_REPO\" in buildEnv),\n   \"LIGHTFAST_DESKTOP_RELEASE_REPO removed from build env\");\n\nfor (const v of [\"APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY\", \"APPLE_TEAM_ID\", \"APPLE_CERT_BASE64\",\n                 \"APPLE_CERT_PASSWORD\", \"APPLE_API_KEY_ID\", \"APPLE_API_ISSUER\",\n                 \"APPLE_API_KEY_CONTENT\", \"KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD\"]) {\n  ok(v in buildEnv, `build job env includes ${v}`);\n}\n\nconst perms = buildJob?.permissions ?? {};\nok(perms[\"id-token\"] === \"write\", \"build job permissions id-token: write\");\nok(perms.attestations === \"write\", \"build job permissions attestations: write\");\n\nconst steps = buildJob?.steps ?? [];\nconst hasSourcemapStep = steps.some((s) => {\n  const run = s.run ?? \"\";\n  return run.includes(\"sourcemaps:upload\");\n});\nok(hasSourcemapStep, \"build job includes sourcemaps:upload step\");\n\nconst hasAttestStep = steps.some((s) => {\n  const uses = s.uses ?? \"\";\n  return uses.includes(\"actions/attest-build-provenance\");\n});\nok(hasAttestStep, \"build job includes attest-build-provenance step\");\n\n// Check attestation subject-path covers .zip\nconst attestStep = steps.find((s) => (s.uses ?? \"\").includes(\"actions/attest-build-provenance\"));\nconst subjectPath = attestStep?.with?.[\"subject-path\"] ?? \"\";\nok(subjectPath.includes(\"apps/desktop/out/make\"),\n   `attestation subject-path targets apps/desktop/out/make (got: ${JSON.stringify(subjectPath)})`);\n\n// Check prepare job has version extraction + release-notes fallback\nconst prepare = jobs.prepare;\nconst prepareSteps = prepare?.steps ?? [];\nconst hasPrevTag = prepareSteps.some((s) => {\n  const run = s.run ?? \"\";\n  return run.includes(\"git tag -l\") && run.includes(\"@lightfast/desktop@\");\n});\nok(hasPrevTag, \"prepare job computes prev tag from @lightfast/desktop@* tags\");\n\nconst hasCheckout = prepareSteps.some((s) => String(s.uses ?? \"\").startsWith(\"actions/checkout@v4\"));\nok(hasCheckout, \"prepare job checks out repo (needed for git tag -l)\");\n\nconst coPrepareCheckout = prepareSteps.find((s) => String(s.uses ?? \"\").startsWith(\"actions/checkout@v4\"));\nok(coPrepareCheckout?.with?.[\"fetch-depth\"] === 0, \"prepare checkout fetch-depth: 0 (full history for tag sort)\");\n\n// Build job produces both arm64 and x64\nconst strategy = buildJob?.strategy?.matrix ?? {};\nconst arches = strategy.arch ?? strategy.architecture ?? [];\nconst archesFlat = Array.isArray(arches) ? arches.map(String) : [];\nok(archesFlat.includes(\"arm64\") && archesFlat.includes(\"x64\"),\n   `matrix covers arm64 + x64 (got: ${JSON.stringify(archesFlat)})`);\n\nconsole.log(fails === 0 ? \"\\nALL PASS\" : `\\n${fails} FAIL`);\nprocess.exit(fails ? 1 : 0);\n"

Here's the final state of the automated manual verification:

## Automated (deterministic, ran locally)

**Phase B — missing-CLERK error** ✓
Ran bundled main directly via `electron .vite/build/bootstrap.js` with `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` unset. t3-env crashed at bundle-load with a readable banner naming the exact variable — not a silent fallback:
```
❌ Invalid environment variables: [
  { path: [ 'NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY' ], message: 'Invalid input' }
]
```

**Phase C — sourcemap upload script** ✓
Intercepted `pnpm exec sentry-cli` by putting a mock `pnpm` first on `PATH` and running `node scripts/upload-sourcemaps.mjs` with fake `SENTRY_*` env. Captured 4 calls in exact order: `releases new` → `upload-sourcemaps .vite/build --url-prefix app:///` → `upload-sourcemaps .vite/renderer/main_window --url-prefix app:///` → `releases finalize`, all keyed to `@lightfast/desktop@0.0.0+1`.

**Phase D — release workflow structure** ✓
Parsed `desktop-release.yml` with `yaml` and asserted: tag trigger `@lightfast/desktop@*`, `cancel-in-progress: true`, arm64+x64 matrix, job-level Sentry+Apple env, step-level Apple cert/keychain env, `id-token: write` + `attestations: write` perms, sourcemaps-upload step, attest-build-provenance step with `apps/desktop/out/make/**/*.zip` subject, prepare-job `fetch-depth: 0` and prev-tag computation.

**Phase E — paths filter** ✓
Simulated GitHub's `paths:` matcher with `picomatch`: 12/12 cases correct. `apps/desktop/**`, `packages/{app-trpc,ui,lib}/**`, `pnpm-lock.yaml`, and the workflow itself trigger; `apps/www/**`, `apps/app/**`, `apps/platform/**`, and other workflows skip.

**Phase F — config invariants** ✓
19/19 checks: `.changeset/config.json.ignore` contains `@lightfast/desktop`; root `package.json` has `dev:desktop-api` and no `dev:desktop-stack`; desktop `package.json` has `clean` + `sourcemaps:upload` scripts, no per-package `lint`, `@sentry/cli` + `@t3-oss/env-core` devdeps; `.env.example` present with all 9 documented vars; `git check-ignore apps/desktop/.env.example` exits 1.

## Not automatable (genuinely require humans/secrets)

- **Phase B items 1–2**: Real Clerk key + packaged-app Sentry event — needs real Clerk publishable key and live Sentry project.
- **Phase C Sentry UI check**: Requires real Sentry auth token.
- **Phase D live run**: Pushing `@lightfast/desktop@0.1.0-rc.1` into the public repo, then verifying codesign/notarize/feed JSON/Sentry artifacts/attestation tab — all require Phase A secrets and intentional publication.
- **Phase E branch protection**: Repo-settings UI config.
- **Phase F sign-in smoke test**: Real Clerk + human at the login window.

The plan diff annotates what was verified; you can `git add/commit` it or discard.

---
import { resolve } from "node:path";
import react from "@vitejs/plugin-react";
import { defineConfig } from "vite";

export default defineConfig({
  root: resolve(import.meta.dirname, "src/renderer"),
  // Load .env.* from the desktop app root (next to package.json), not from
  // src/renderer — otherwise VITE_* vars are silently undefined and
  // entry.tsx falls back to the prod base URL.
  envDir: resolve(import.meta.dirname),
  plugins: [react()],
  resolve: {
    // Follow pnpm symlinks to real paths so esbuild's optimizer resolves
    // transitive CJS peers (e.g. copy-anything imported by superjson) via the
    // .pnpm store layout instead of getting stuck in symlinked node_modules
    // that lack the peer.
    preserveSymlinks: false,
  },
  optimizeDeps: {
    include: [
      "@repo/app-trpc/desktop",
      "@repo/app-trpc/react",
      "superjson",
      "sonner",
    ],
  },
  build: {
    outDir: resolve(import.meta.dirname, ".vite/renderer/main_window"),
    emptyOutDir: true,
    rollupOptions: {
      input: resolve(import.meta.dirname, "src/renderer/index.html"),
    },
  },
});

import { join } from "node:path";
import { app } from "electron";
import squirrelStartup from "electron-squirrel-startup";
import { mainEnv } from "../env/main";

if (squirrelStartup) {
  app.quit();
}

const productName = app.isPackaged ? "Lightfast" : "Lightfast Dev";
app.setName(productName);
app.setPath("userData", join(app.getPath("appData"), productName));

if (!app.isPackaged) {
  const port = mainEnv.LIGHTFAST_REMOTE_DEBUG_PORT;
  if (port !== undefined) {
    app.commandLine.appendSwitch("remote-debugging-port", String(port));
    app.commandLine.appendSwitch("remote-debugging-address", "127.0.0.1");
    console.log(`[cdp] remote debugging on 127.0.0.1:${port}`);
  }
}

if (!app.requestSingleInstanceLock()) {
  app.exit(0);
}

import("./index").catch((error) => {
  console.error("Failed to load main module", error);
  app.exit(1);
});

> AGENT

Let me plan this quickly before touching things.

**Approach**:
1. Copy `apps/app/.vercel/.env.development.local` into worktree at `apps/desktop/.vercel/.env.development.local` (git-ignored, matches monorepo convention — CLAUDE.md says env lives at `apps/<app>/.vercel/.env.development.local`).
2. Add `with-env` + `dotenv-cli` to `apps/desktop/package.json`, mirroring `apps/app`'s pattern. This loads the dotenv into the parent shell's `process.env` before `electron-forge start`, which is what Electron's main process inherits.
3. `pnpm install` to pick up `dotenv-cli`.
4. Run `pnpm dev:desktop` to verify the app boots end-to-end.

This is a small functional change to desktop's dev wiring — it fills a real gap in the plan (plan assumed `.env.development` auto-loaded, but nothing in the codebase actually does that).
